Refusing to fold, Qianli Xun switched gears fast—reading gaps between his heavy swings, darting inside his reach with lightning footwork and razor-accurate counters that turned his own momentum against him, throwing his balance off and hammering exposed pressure points until the giant began to flag!
Exhales of relief escaped Matron Wei's group.
Panic crept into Chen Haoyu's eyes—who knew fragile porcelain hid iron springs underneath?!
Meanwhile the village head prayed silent: Heaven let Proprietor win—rent-free forever, happy days ahead!
Staggering back two steps off balance, the giant left his gut wide open—Qianli Xun shot in and drove a crisp palm-backed punch straight into his solar plexus, dropping him flat in the dirt! Drawing back her fists, breathing hard, a triumphant grin lit her face.
Struggling up off the dust, the bruiser dropped straight onto both knees before her: "Master above—accept your disciple's bow!"
Having trained plenty of male students in her old world—none looking this rough around the edges—Qianli Xun waved him off: "Get up! Not taking apprentices!"
Climbing to his feet, the fighter declared firm: "Refuse me or not, I still call you Master! My name is Lu Yuanfeng—till we meet again!" Nursing his aching ribs, Lu Yuanfeng limped away.
Chen Haoyu stood frozen like a wooden post—not only had his ace lost, he'd bowed down calling Zhu Shuzhen Master! Biggest humiliation of Chen Haoyu's entire life!
"Village Head! Brush, ink, and paper!" Qianli Xun called brisk. "Drawing up a new contract right here—Young Master Chen and I sign and thumbprint together!"
Beaming from ear to ear, the village head rushed out paper, brush, and red ink paste. Qianli Xun drafted the terms crisp, signed her name, pressed her thumbprint, and turned to Chen Haoyu: "Your turn—all three conditions, zero backing out!"
Seething yet cornered, Chen Haoyu stalked up, scrawled his name, and slammed down his thumbprint. "Don't think luck stays with you forever, Zhu Shuzhen—the day you fall into my hands, you'll beg for mercy!" Snatching his copy, he turned to leave with his thugs.
Scampering up to block him with a bow, the village head beamed: "Young Master Chen—village rent is really waived forever?!"
"Written right on the contract—think I'd stiff you?! Get out of my face!" Chen Haoyu snarled.
"Yes, yes, getting out of your face! Safe travels, Young Master Chen!" Then he spun round showering bows and blessings upon Matron Wei and their former proprietor.
"Save the speeches, Village Head," Qianli Xun directed. "Grab four men to carry those two silver chests down to the carriage at the village entrance—Matron Wei heads back to town today."
"Done, former Proprietor!" Calling four sturdy villagers at once, he had both chests hauled out to the road where Matron Wei's page boys loaded them safe aboard her carriage.
With grove business wrapped clean, Matron Wei prepared to depart; Qianli Xun walked her out to the village edge.
Matron Wei clasped her hand with warm advice: "Witnessed your fighting prowess firsthand now—feel much safer leaving you out here, doubt Young Master Chen dares bother you again. Still, listen to an old woman: meddle less in other folks' fights! Strong as you are, you can't rescue everyone under Heaven—take care of yourself first."
"Good advice, Matron—I'll keep it in mind."
Gazing at her with fond pride, Matron Wei smiled: "Changed as much as you have, your kind heart stayed pure—wonderful!"
Seeing Matron Wei off, Qianli Xun strolled back to her bamboo cottage. Whether this bamboo grove had worked mystical luck yet or not, her mind felt calmer and clearer every single day—fueling fresh creative flow! She resolved to spend the remaining weeks pouring herself into writing huaben scripts.
#CH 40
#TITLE: Chapter 40: Arriving in Lin'an, Visiting Qiao Manor
Leaving Prince Yi's Manor in Bianliang under Matron Zou's care, Shen Jianchen packed his bags and set off south for Lin'an with Qiao Mengrong.
Dressed in neat guard attire, hair combed crisp, face scrubbed spotless revealing sharp, handsome features, Bai Feng looked like a whole different kid.
Ever since taking him on, Prince Yi made time every single evening after checking into roadside inns to lead Bai Feng to quiet courtyards and drill Shen Fist move by move.
Quick-witted and athletic, Bai Feng picked up forms fast—though Prince Yi reminded him true Shen Fist power grew only from daily sweat and persistence.
Outside martial training, Bai Feng waited on His Highness's daily needs along the road.
Princess Jiahe had urged bringing several personal maids to tend Brother's comfort, only for him to turn every one down.
Looking over those fresh, lively maids, Bai Feng felt it was a crying shame and even nudged Prince Yi to bring at least one so he'd have company to chat with on the long journey—only for Prince Yi to insist Bai Feng alone was plenty, convincing Bai Feng his master had zero interest in women!
The caravan rolled south in three carriages: Qiao Wenxian with his attendants in one; Prince Yi and Bai Feng in the second; Princess Jiahe and Xilan in the third.
Time and again Qiao Mengrong longed to ride in her brother's carriage—hoping to lean against his shoulder, maybe even feel his arm slip around her—yet every time he clung tight to Bai Feng, giving her zero opening.
Once she asked outright, only for him to decline flat: bumpy roads, rough jouncing, clumsy guy like him wouldn't take good care of her—better let Xilan tend her!
An excuse full of holes that stung Qiao Mengrong's heart, leaving her riding quietly with Xilan.
Spotting Princess Jiahe's downcast eyes, Bai Feng felt bad and offered inside the carriage to swap seats with the princess—earning a sharp reminder from Prince Yi to mind his guard duties and stay out of private matters!
Private matters?! This was His Highness's future bride! Xilan had already told Bai Feng they were wed next Mid-Autumn Festival—who turns down riding with their own fiancée?! Total mystery to Bai Feng!
After just over half a month on the road, Lin'an rose ahead.
Smooth sailing without a single bandit sighting—almost disappointing Shen Jianchen, who had half hoped to cross paths with gallant outlaws like Mount Liang heroes and befriend them!
In no hurry to report for duty at the prefecture office yet, he planned to grab a few free days roaming town hunting for Qianli Xun.
The fortune-teller pointed to places tied to the "mouth"—so restaurants, wine taverns, and teahouses topped his list!
Less sprawling than Bianliang yet every bit as vibrant, Lin'an radiated southern grace—delicate timber architecture, bustling water-town lanes, specialty shops, graceful stone arches curving over canals.
At Prince Yi's nod, Qiao Wenxian led the convoy straight to Qiao Manor first.
Though not top one or two, the Qiao family ranked squarely in Lin'an's top five great houses.
Beyond ancestral roots, their eldest daughter had entered the palace and borne Prince Yi; though she died young and her son stayed sickly, imperial kinship lent prestige that made everyone in Lin'an defer three steps.
Family patriarch Qiao Shouren stood past sixty. Besides his late eldest daughter, he had four more daughters: the second and third borne by primary wife Madam Qian, both already wed.
In his forties, anxious for a male heir to carry ancestral incense, Qiao Shouren listened to Madam Qian and took a concubine surnamed Mi—near the eldest daughter's age—who delighted the whole clan by bearing Qiao Wenxian within two years!
Destined to inherit the Qiao estate, Qiao Wenxian was raised at Madam Qian's side. Still young and hoping for more sons, Concubine Mi hadn't minded—only to bear two more daughters, both still awaiting marriage. Brief resentment faded fast because Madam Qian stayed warm, fair, and never drove a wedge between mother and son, letting both women get along in genuine harmony.
Rolling south through lively streets, the three carriages halted outside Qiao Manor's main gate. Doormen rushed forward grinning: "Welcome home, Young Master!"
"Run tell Father—Prince Yi has arrived!" Qiao Wenxian instructed.
Casting an awed look past him, a servant dashed inside.
Leading Prince Yi and the others through the gates, they faced sculpted rockery and flowing water screening inner courtyards from street view. Rounding the boulders revealed graceful halls across a short covered walkway spanning clear water.
A whole welcoming party hurried out led by Qiao Shouren—trim, dignified, neatly groomed silver-streaked hair—flanked by Madam Qian and Concubine Mi, with two unmarried daughters trailing behind.
Seeing Prince Yi, the entire family dropped to their knees in formal kowtow.
Used to servants kneeling by now yet jolted seeing family elders kneel to him, Shen Jianchen rushed forward lifting Qiao Shouren by both arms.
"Grandfather, Grandmothers, and my two..."
Stuck mid-sentence—those two girls looked even younger than him despite outranking him as maternal aunts!—he skipped titles smooth: "We're all family! Since when does kin kneel to kin? Especially elders kneeling to a junior—everyone get up!"
Laughing warm, the household rose.
Looking Prince Yi up and down in wonder, Qiao Shouren beamed: "When servants announced Prince Yi, I thought I misheard—how could you travel all this way sick? Seeing you hail and hearty with my own eyes proves Heaven's blessing—joy for the Qiao house, and boundless luck for Princess Jiahe!"
Hearing her name, Qiao Mengrong stepped forward with a graceful curtsy: "Following Brother's lead—Grandfather, Grandmothers, and... these two ladies look my own age, surely I can't call them aunts?!"
Giggling shyly, one girl cried: "Please don't call me Aunt, Princess Jiahe—I'd die of embarrassment!"
Madam Qian smiled warm: "Generations say one thing, yet with Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe's exalted rank, call them simply by name!"
Qiao Wenxian stepped in to introduce them: "Tall one's Wenhui, shorter one's Wenxiu."
In unison Wenhui and Wenxiu curtsied: "Greetings to Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe!"
"Father, they're worn out from half a month on the road—let's talk indoors," Qiao Wenxian urged.
"Right, right—Wenyuan's right! Come inside, Prince Yi, Princess Jiahe!"
Inside the reception hall flowed warm family talk—Bianliang anecdotes, tender memories of late Consort Qiao cut short lest they sadden the prince.
Seeing it was Prince Yi's first trip to Lin'an, Qiao Shouren briefed him on family affairs and urged him not to buy a separate residence: the Qiao family owned a scenic estate just outside the city walls—close enough for daily errands, ideal for Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe!
Gladly accepting, Shen Jianchen also shared his appointment as Military Commissioner of Lin'an—thrilling Qiao Shouren and the whole clan, knowing imperial favor lifted the Qiao house too.
After long conversation they broke up: Wenhui and Wenxiu escorted Princess Jiahe to guest rooms, while Qiao Wenxian guided Prince Yi to his quarters.
Inside, Bai Feng offered to unpack—only to find every necessity already spotless and ready, so he begged leave to roam Lin'an's streets!
"Go on—be back early," Shen Jianchen waved him off; in a flash Bai Feng bolted out.
Qiao Wenxian shook his head: "No idea why you keep Bai Feng around—zero discipline, runs off playing nonstop!"
"Room has everything already—no chores left, why trap him indoors?"
"Lucky rascal landing a boss like you!"
Mid-laugh, Nan Shuang burst in and threw both arms around Qiao Wenxian: "Young Master! Heard you were home the second I walked in and ran straight here! Nearly a whole year—missed you to death!"
Shen Jianchen burst out laughing: "Look who's talking about discipline—your own study attendant tackles you through the door!"
"My Nan Shuang beats Bai Feng ten times over!" Chuckling helpless, Qiao Wenxian nudged him off: "Too warm for bear hugs—let go!"
Releasing him, Nan Shuang whined: "Missed you, Master! Whole time you were gone I never got to see the world—stuck camping outside Lin Manor gate every day, boring as dirt!"
"Quit babbling and bow to Prince Yi!"
Nan Shuang dropped into a quick bow: "Greetings, Prince Yi!"
"Why'd your master make you camp outside Lin Manor?" Shen Jianchen asked.
"Your Highness doesn't know—my master adores Youqi Jushi's poetry! When she married into Lin Manor and Master left for Bianliang, he posted me outside their gate collecting her discarded manuscript scraps!"
Youqi Jushi—that had to be Zhu Shuzhen! Wenyuan really had it bad!
Unbothered by Nan Shuang spilling everything, Qiao Wenxian cut straight to business: "Camping out there all this time, did you ever glimpse Youqi Jushi?"
"Never once—only her maid Kuqiao."
Stepping to the desk, Qiao Wenxian penned a quick note on ornamental paper, folded it neat, and held it out: "Deliver this to Kuqiao—tell her I beg a brief meeting with Youqi Jushi."
Nan Shuang didn't reach for it: "Save your ink, Master—Youqi Jushi isn't even at Lin Manor right now! Heard from Lin servants she's turned super fierce—fought Training Officer Lin and moved out to the countryside for a whole month! Counting days, she's been gone almost three weeks—wait till she's back to invite her."
Crumpling the ornamental sheet and tossing it into the waste tub, Qiao Wenxian looked crestfallen: "Turned fierce—what happened?"
"Just what Lin servants whispered: early on the Training Officer beat her black and blue all the time, then out of nowhere she changed like a whole new person—got tough enough even the Training Officer fears her!"
Qiao Wenxian let out a bitter sigh: "Gentle scholar forced into survival by a brutal husband—my late grandmother spoke truth: a wife can rarely change a husband, yet a husband changes a wife overnight! Want to know a man's character, just look at his wife!"
Listening beside him, Shen Jianchen paid little mind—his thoughts stayed glued to Qianli Xun, leaving zero headspace for Youqi Jushi's marriage drama. Still, seeing Wenyuan's glum face, he offered comfort: "Cheer up—toughening up is good! Out in the countryside at least Lin Jue can't hit her. Invite her out once she gets back."
"True enough—no sense fretting. Tell me your plan: when do you reveal your title as Military Commissioner?"
"Tomorrow we move into your family's suburban villa. Then walk me through every restaurant, teahouse, wine tavern, and Goulan Wasi in Lin'an—once I've seen enough, I'll show my seal."
"Not scared His Majesty gets mad?"
"His Majesty sits way up in Bianliang—how's he know when I reached Lin'an? Even if he finds out, excuse ready: sick indoors all my life, finally well, took a few days sightseeing! He'll understand."
"Got all the angles covered! How long do you plan roaming town?"
"Haven't decided—not too long, got official work waiting."
"Good you remember."
Meanwhile, settled comfortably into her guest courtyard by Wenhui and Wenxiu, Qiao Mengrong sat by the window staring at a phoenix tree outside. Past bloom, its canopy hung thick with broad green leaves big as her brother's palms—yet beauty escaped her. Aside from taking office, Brother had come to Lin'an searching for someone... WHO? Could he really not be Zhao Pu?
She had known every soul in Zhao Pu's entire world—yet this awakened brother sat right beside her like an unsolvable riddle.
#CH 41
#TITLE: Chapter 41: Youlin Retreat and the Divine Willow
Next morning Shen Jianchen left Qiao Manor for the Qiao family's suburban retreat, sharing a carriage with Qiao Wenxian; Bai Feng drove Prince Yi's carriage while Nan Shuang drove Princess Jiahe's.
With a stretch of country road ahead, Qiao Wenxian struck up conversation: "Notice you aren't nearly as warm toward Princess Jiahe as before, Prince Yi—what gives? Amnesia really wiped out all your love for her?"
Shen Jianchen nodded.
"Poor Princess Jiahe! While you lay sick, she waited on you hand and foot—even watching from the side made me envious! Don't let amnesia make you break her heart."
Words caught in Shen Jianchen's throat—pining for Qianli Xun had already betrayed Zhao Pu's love for Mengrong! What choice did he have when his heart belonged to Qianli Xun? Quietly he had drawn a line: if he never found Qianli Xun, fine, he'd honor Zhao Pu's wish and marry Mengrong—without Qianli Xun, marrying anyone was the same; yet if he DID find Qianli Xun, he was choosing her—though how to untangle things with Mengrong left him stumped.
Seeing Prince Yi fall silent, Qiao Wenxian dropped the subject.
Flipping the table, Shen Jianchen asked: "Quit lecturing me—Youqi Jushi is a married woman and you're still chasing her and sending secret invites! Ever think that's kinda sketchy?"
Qiao Wenxian fired up at once: "I loved her FIRST, she married AFTER! Inviting her out is strictly to help her—never once crossed a single proper line! Don't throw morality lectures at me!"
"If you loved her first, why didn't you propose earlier? Marry her yourself and none of this mess happens!"
Qiao Wenxian let out a bitter sigh: "Back when I loved her, her heart belonged to someone else—how could I push my parents to propose?! Then that bastard Training Officer Lin jumped ahead, and Prefect Zhu actually agreed! Nearly tore my guts out watching her wed another man—fled straight to Bianliang heartbroken!"
"Prefect Zhu? Her father is Prefect of Lin'an Zhu Xiyan?!"
"The very one!"
"Her dad's the Prefect—why didn't he stop her husband from beating her?!"
"Maybe she never told him."
"Wait—up in Bianliang, how did YOU know her husband beat her?"
"Far away didn't mean I stopped caring—I wrote Dongfang Chenze asking him to keep me posted on how she fared after marriage."
"Got it. Rough deal though—one-sided love all along."
"Yep. She knew my feelings, yet with another man in her heart she couldn't accept me."
"Ever think even if she divorces Lin Jue, she still might not pick you if she loves that other guy?"
Qiao Wenxian brightened: "That guy moved away years ago, married, already has kids—zero chance for them! My mind's set: the day she divorces, I tell my parents straight and send them proposing to Zhu Manor!"
"Already been married once—doesn't bother you?"
"Truly love someone, why would that ever matter?"
Moved by Wenyuan's fierce devotion, Shen Jianchen silently vowed: whatever roadblocks stood between Wenyuan and love, he'd clear every last one!
Soon the carriages reached the edge of dense woods—close enough to Lin'an that once he took office, commuting to the prefecture yamen would be a breeze.
Tucked deep inside thick forest, the retreat stayed invisible from afar; only up close did a tidy dirt lane cut through the trees wide enough for a single carriage, graded special by the Qiao family.
Pulling up before the entrance, everyone stepped down beneath a plaque reading "Youlin Retreat"—fitting to a T.
The gate swung open as a steward hurried out bowing: "Prince Yi, Princess, Young Master! Master sent word yesterday you'd be moving in today, so I had servants scrub every hall inside and out overnight! Anything lacking, just say the word and I'll fix it at once!"
"Good work, Butler Li—show Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe to their quarters first," Qiao Wenxian directed.
"Right this way, Your Highness, Princess, Young Master!" Butler Li led them in.
First visit here himself, Qiao Wenxian looked around delighted. Half the size of Qiao Manor, its genius lay in folding wild woods straight inside the estate walls: right past the gate stood three rows of live trees forming a living screen wall, stone paths curving around both sides amid untouched woodland.
Beyond the green screen stood more forest echoing with birdsong before graceful pavilions appeared in small clearings, each courtyard cushioned from the next by living trees.
"Main hall right here for Prince Yi," Butler Li announced. "Behind it sits a private ladies' courtyard for Princess Jiahe—both spotless! Let me show the Princess back."
Enchanted by the woodland haven, Qiao Wenxian changed plans on the spot: "Wait—I'm staying at the retreat too! Set up a courtyard for me, Butler Li!"
Butler Li chuckled: "Empty courtyards galore—aside from the main hall and rear ladies' wing, pick any you like, every room's cleaned!"
"Nan Shuang!" Qiao Wenxian called at once. "Run back to Qiao Manor and haul my daily gear over—may not be fairyland, yet this place oozes charm! I'm keeping Prince Yi company right here!"
"Yes, Master!" Nan Shuang tugged Bai Feng's sleeve: "Come help me haul!"
Bai Feng balked: "No way—I have to wait on His Highness!"
"Go lend Nan Shuang a hand—I'm fine here," Shen Jianchen waved him off.
"Since His Highness says so, fine—let's go back to Qiao Manor!"
Once the two lads left, Butler Li guided Qiao Mengrong along stone flagstones curving behind the main hall into a secluded courtyard ringed with emerald foliage—she fell in love with it on sight.
"All maids assigned and ready, Princess—need anything, send your personal maid for me. Excuse me while I see which courtyard Young Master picks."
Eager to arrange the rooms to her taste with Xilan, Qiao Mengrong smiled: "Go tend your work, Butler Li—I'm all set."
East of the main hall, Qiao Wenxian picked a cozy smaller courtyard—won over by a raised wooden platform built right outside its wall!
Engineered across five towering, stout tree trunks—four at the corners, one central—its pruned crowns supported sturdy timber decking ringed with railings while lush outer boughs spread leafy shade all around.
"Never knew my stiff old man had such flair!" Qiao Wenxian grinned. "Come climb the tree platform with me for the view, Prince Yi!"
Game at once, Shen Jianchen climbed the timber stairs right behind him.
Rock-solid decking—not even a creak underfoot—held a round wooden table and two chairs, ideal for sipping tea over endless green canopy.
"Breathtaking!" Qiao Wenxian breathed. "Floating right on top of the forest looking out over every tree!"
Strolling up below, Butler Li called upward: "Picked your courtyard, Young Master?!"
"Right here!" Qiao Wenxian shouted back down.
"Do you and Prince Yi like it?!"
Shen Jianchen stayed quiet while Qiao Wenxian boomed: "Love it! Go on about your work, don't mind us!"
Chuckling as he walked off, Butler Li muttered to himself: "First visit, all starry-eyed—living out here every day, I'm sick of how dead quiet it is compared to town!"
Leaning against the railing, Shen Jianchen scanned past the treetops toward a green mountain rising nearby: "What mountain is that?"
Glancing over, Qiao Wenxian shrugged: "Nameless hill—though the nunnery halfway up is famous because its abbess supposedly sees past and future."
"People actually buy that?"
"Word on the street—just repeating what everyone says."
Still gazing toward the hill, a blazing splash of crimson at its base snagged Shen Jianchen's eye: "What's that red down there?"
Following his finger, Qiao Wenxian smiled: "Oh, a Millennial Divine Willow! Young couples in Lin'an hike down from the nunnery after praying for love and tie red silk ribbons onto its branches—legend says the tree grew from a single whisker of the Old Man Under the Moon!"
"Millennial Divine Willow!" Heart hammering, Shen Jianchen gripped the rail: "Wenyuan—take me over there right now!"
"Sure—just a short walk through the woods!"
Descending the platform and stepping out of Youlin Retreat, they followed a natural forest trail winding beneath thick canopy.
Stepping out of the trees into bright open sky, a tranquil pond spread right before them.
Heat stung Shen Jianchen's eyes—this pond mirrored the exact water from his old life so close he half thought he had jumped home, until Wenyuan's silhouette beside him anchored reality.
Walking step by slow step around the pond toward the ancient willow on the far shore, pulse thundering harder every pace—this very tree had crashed over Qianli Xun and him during the quake and hurled him across a millennium!
Puzzled by Prince Yi's raw emotion yet too tactful to pry, Qiao Wenxian trailed quiet behind.
Reaching the trunk, a single glistening tear spilled off Prince Yi's cheek onto a grass blade below like morning dew—making Qiao Wenxian wonder: First time seeing this willow, why is he moved to tears?
Then Prince Yi stepped right up and wrapped both arms around the thick bark, bewildering him further.
Pressing his forehead against the trunk, Shen Jianchen pleaded in silence: You brought me to this century, Divine Willow—tell me where Qianli Xun is!
Unable to hold back, Qiao Wenxian asked: "Prince Yi, are you alright?"
Startled back to himself, embarrassed at breaking down, Shen Jianchen let go, blinked back stinging moisture, and turned with a quick cover: "Fine—just saw a willow identical to this in dreams over and over; felt uncanny up close."
"Aha!" Gazing up at hundreds of red silk ribbons fluttering in the breeze, Qiao Wenxian spotted one lone ribbon tied blazing high in the top boughs: "Whoa—who on earth tied a ribbon WAY up there?! How tall a ladder did they haul?!"
Stepping back to squint upward, Shen Jianchen replied: "No ladder—whoever tied it knows light-foot skill! Vaulted straight up and balanced on the branches."
"Someone that skilled?!" Qiao Wenxian marveled.
Shen Jianchen scoffed playful: "No big deal—I can do that too."
"Prove it! Show me!"
Kicking off with light-foot skill, Shen Jianchen shot effortlessly up into the high canopy, standing light as a feather on a slender swaying limb.
"Well? Impressed?"
Staring up in awe, Qiao Wenxian called: "Divine sage teach you that too?!"
"Course!" Shen Jianchen grinned smug.
"Since you're right next to it, peek at what's written on that red ribbon!"
Glancing sideways at the fluttering silk, Shen Jianchen shook his head: "Pass—somebody else's private wish, none of our business!" Dropping smooth back to earth, he landed neat before Qiao Wenxian.
"Boring! Jumped all the way up for nothing!" Qiao Wenxian groaned, craning his neck at the high silk in disappointment.
Clapping his shoulder, Shen Jianchen laughed: "Come on—tomorrow you're showing me every corner of Lin'an!"
Heading back into the woods, Shen Jianchen vowed he'd visit this willow often.
At the forest edge he threw one last glance back across the pond: If you're here in this era, Divine Willow, Qianli Xun has to be here too—and I WILL find her!
#CH 42
#TITLE: Chapter 42: Searching the Streets and Juxiang Tower
Right after breakfast at Youlin Retreat next morning, Shen Jianchen and Qiao Wenxian rode into bustling Lin'an.
At the edge of the market quarter, Nan Shuang hitched their carriage at a guarded livery yard where attendants watched rigs for a few copper coins.
Thrilled to hit town, Bai Feng trailed behind Prince Yi and Young Master Qiao alongside Nan Shuang, soaking up every sight.
"Lively enough, sure—still can't touch Bianliang! Different stuff on the stalls too," Bai Feng commented.
Nan Shuang scoffed: "Ever heard of local flavor?! What's the fun if Lin'an copied Bianliang?!"
Following their masters into a busy teahouse, they stepped inside.
A waiter jumped up beaming: "Right inside, young masters!"
Taking a table, Qiao Wenxian ordered a pot of brewed tea.
Eyeing his master sip tea while sweeping sharp eyes over every single face in the room, Bai Feng thought today's outing felt odd—especially His Highness warning him before leaving never to call him Prince Yi, only "Young Master Shen."
"Looking for somebody, Young Master?" Bai Feng blurted out.
If even Bai Feng noticed, Wenyuan couldn't miss it! Shen Jianchen covered brusque: "Drink your tea and cut the chatter."
Half a pot down, restless already, Shen Jianchen steered Wenyuan out to a wine tavern.
At the tavern, Qiao Wenxian ordered a small flagon of Capital Spring: "Lin'an's signature vintage, Young Master Shen—one sip and you'll love it!"
Barely listening, Shen Jianchen scanned every booth and patron.
Irritated now, Qiao Wenxian called him out: "Acting like that, even I know you're hunting someone! Who is it—can't you tell me?"
How could he explain Qianli Xun? Wenyuan wouldn't buy transmigration and would only lecture him about breaking Princess Jiahe's heart!
Back at Youlin Retreat that morning, Qiao Mengrong had begged to come along; dreading dragging a delicate lady through a full day's foot search, he had brushed her off: "Long, exhausting errands today—way too tiring for you! Next stroll I'll definitely bring you."
Her eyes had dropped with quiet hurt: "Come home early, Brother."
Nodding promise, guilt had pricked him once more—funny how every time he faced Mengrong, guilt crept straight in.
"Earth to Shen—what are you spacing out on?" Qiao Wenxian poked.
Snapping back, Shen Jianchen shrugged: "Searching for who? First time in Lin'an, you're the only soul I know! Just curious gawking at a new city!"
Leaning close, Qiao Wenxian squinted: "Scanning every corner like hunting a long-lost sweetheart—mighty suspicious!"
"Quit imagining things—drink!"
Lifting his cup, he tasted Capital Spring—rich, mellow, lingering sweet. "Great wine! Fill me up, Bai Feng!"
Bai Feng poured straight to the brim.
Maybe the fine wine cleared his head: chasing fate too hard only made it slip through your fingers, whereas unexpected wonders bloomed when you relaxed! Hawkeyeing every stranger just spooked his companions and wouldn't magically spot Qianli Xun anyway—better chill out and trust destiny!
Split four ways among him, Wenyuan, Bai Feng, and Nan Shuang, the small flagon vanished fast without leaving a buzz.
Before Qiao Wenxian could order a second pot, Shen Jianchen waved him off: "Enough tavern—let's walk the streets and hit a restaurant for lunch."
"Perfect! Stroll straight north out of here, browse shops along the way, and right when hunger hits we land smack at Juxiang Tower!"
Music to Shen Jianchen's ears.
Leaving the tavern, they strolled north through lively avenues.
Bai Feng and Nan Shuang had a blast pointing at every novelty, while the two gentlemen chatted—yet Shen Jianchen's eyes still drifted automatically across passing faces.
Then he almost smacked his own forehead: idiot! Crossing over had changed HIS face—if Qianli Xun crossed over too, she'd wear someone else's face! Hunting by looks was hopeless! A quiet sigh slipped out: "Ah..."
"Why the sigh, Young Master Shen?"
"Just thinking how unpredictable life is."
"Bustling market streets make you brood over fleeting life?"
"Brighter the boom, deeper the hush once the party ends."
Baffled by the prince's sudden gloom—and more focused on his own growling stomach—Qiao Wenxian pointed ahead: "Just a bit further to Juxiang Tower!"
"Food actually good at Juxiang Tower, Young Master Qiao?" Bai Feng piped up.
Before Wenyuan spoke, Nan Shuang bragged: "Is Juxiang Tower good?! Top restaurant in all of Lin'an—my master eats there nonstop!"
"Better than Fan Tower back in Bianliang?" Bai Feng challenged.
Never having been to Bianliang, Nan Shuang went mute.
"Each has its own glory," Qiao Wenxian mediated with a smile. "Ever actually eaten at Fan Tower, Bai Feng?"
"Well, no... just heard folks in Bianliang raving about Fan Tower all day, so it stuck!"
Nan Shuang scoffed: "Never even ate there and still bragging?! Showing off you're from Bianliang? Well, never been to Bianliang myself, yet I've feasted at Juxiang Tower plenty thanks to my master—guaranteed heavenly!"
Mouth already watering, Bai Feng sidled up to Prince Yi: "Young Master—do I get to try Juxiang Tower too?"
Shen Jianchen chuckled: "Relax—eat till you burst!"
Bantering along, they reached Juxiang Tower. A doorman darted out beaming: "Young Master Qiao! Long time no see!" Turning indoors he hollered: "Young Master Qiao has arrived!"
Instantly a waiter hurried forward: "Welcome inside, Young Master Qiao! Your upstairs private room is always ready!"
Stepping in behind Wenyuan, Shen Jianchen took in the spacious ground floor: twenty-odd lively tables packed with walk-in diners, while upstairs housed exclusive private rooms reserved for VIPs.
Ducking into a closed room upstairs meant seeing nobody—and if fate really brought Qianli Xun near, hiding behind walls might miss every clue!
"Skip the private room, Wenyuan."
"Upstairs is quiet, private, zero interruptions—much nicer for talking!"
"New in Lin'an—want to rub elbows with locals and soak up local flavor!"
"Inside a restaurant? What local flavor?"
"Every gesture and laugh of Lin'an diners differs from Bianliang—that IS local flavor!"
Outvoted, Qiao Wenxian yielded: "Fine, guest's choice—downstairs it is."
The waiter seated them at a tucked-away ground-floor table.
Qiao Wenxian ordered five signature feasts—Sister Song's Fish Soup, Dongpo pork, Longjing Shrimp, Hangzhou Chrysanthemum Shredded Chicken, and Crispy Fried Tofu Rolls—plus crisp greens and a pot of West Lake Longjing tea.
Just hearing the menu had Bai Feng and Nan Shuang practically drooling on the table!
Scanning the dining hall, Shen Jianchen noticed a burly man slightly older than himself seated at a far table, eating away while glaring straight toward them—too distant to read his thoughts.
"Wenyuan, guy over there keeps staring at us—know him?"
Glancing over, Qiao Wenxian let out a cold snort: "Hmph! That's Youqi Jushi's husband—Lin Jue, Lin'an's Training Officer! Your future subordinate!"
Shen Jianchen smirked playful: "Barely back in Lin'an, haven't even seen Zhu Shuzhen yet, and you run straight into her husband! How's that feel?"
"Feels like punching his lights out!"
"Guy's just sitting there eating—pick a fight and you look petty! Plus look at your frames side by side: three moves and he'd mop the floor with you!"
"Hey—whose side are you even on?!"
"Yours—just keeping you from doing something dumb!"
Hot platters arrived in a wave along with steaming West Lake Longjing tea.
Jumping up, Bai Feng poured tea for Prince Yi, Young Master Qiao, Nan Shuang, and himself before locking hungry eyes on the dishes.
Laughing at his starving look, Shen Jianchen waved: "Dig in!"
Chopsticks flew—Bai Feng and Nan Shuang shoveling like racers!
"Slow down, you two—nobody's stealing your plates!" Shen Jianchen chuckled.
Over at his table, Lin Jue's blood boiled. Even before proposing to Zhu Manor he knew Qiao Wenxian coveted Zhu Shuzhen; after the wedding he heard Qiao fled heartbroken—now suddenly he pops right back in Lin'an?! Heard about the divorce mess?! Worse, Zhu Shuzhen had moved out to the countryside humbling Lin Jue's pride—what if "countryside" was a cover to sneak around with Qiao Wenxian?! Showing up right while she's gone—what else could it mean?!
Slamming down his wine cup, Lin Jue hailed a waiter for a second cup, filled both to the brim, rose, and stalked straight toward Qiao Wenxian...
Mid-bite of Dongpo pork, Shen Jianchen nudged Wenyuan's elbow: "Incoming!"
Chopsticks hovering over shrimp, Qiao Wenxian looked up straight into Lin Jue's eyes; setting down his chopsticks, he met the glare cold as sparks crackled between rivals.
Shen Jianchen glanced at Bai Feng: "Take the hint—Wenyuan's got company! Grab two plates each and move to the next table!"
Scooping two meat platters apiece, Bai Feng and Nan Shuang hopped one table over and kept chowing down without missing a beat.
Dropping into a chair, Lin Jue slid one cup before Qiao Wenxian: "When did Young Master Qiao stroll back into Lin'an?"
"Didn't realize my travel dates needed reporting to the Training Officer," Qiao Wenxian fired back, refusing to yield an inch.
Raising his cup, Lin Jue taunted: "Call yourself a man, drink a few with me!"
Cool and cutting, Qiao Wenxian quoted: "Liu Yi Jushi wrote: 'A thousand cups are too few with a bosom friend; half a word is too much when views clash.' [TN: Translator's rendering] Sums up my mood right now—for me, Training Officer, half a word is already too much!"
Ouyang Xiu's barb lit Lin Jue's fuse—slam!—cup crashing down, splashing wine across half the table!
"Think tossing fancy verses fools everybody?! I see straight through your sanctimonious mask—sleazy hypocrite coveting another man's wife! Mark my word, one day I'll rip that mask off and show everyone what a famed Jiangnan scholar really looks like!"
Furious, Qiao Wenxian shot to his feet: "Slanderous garbage!"
Shooting up too, Lin Jue yanked Qiao Wenxian by the collar: "Spill it—did you sneak back here to shack up with her out in the countryside?!"
Refusing to dignify the filth: "Why should I tell you a thing?!"
Rage boiling over, Lin Jue cocked a heavy fist straight at Qiao Wenxian's jaw...
Instantly Bai Feng and Nan Shuang froze mid-bite, Nan Shuang going pale in terror for his master.
At one subtle flick of Shen Jianchen's eyes, Bai Feng shot forward and clamped onto Lin Jue's swinging fist! Fresh to Shen Fist though he was, blocking one raw punch from Lin Jue took zero strain.
Stopped dead mid-swing, Lin Jue realized real fighters sat at this table! Releasing Qiao Wenxian's collar, he shot a sharp glance at Shen Jianchen—clearly this calm stranger had trained that kid; outnumbered against pros, brawling meant getting crushed!
Seeing Lin Jue back off, Bai Feng released his wrist and strolled right back to his plates beside Nan Shuang.
Lin Jue sneered down at Qiao Wenxian: "Got good news and bad news for you, Qiao Wenxian—which first?"
Sitting back down, Qiao Wenxian ignored him cold.
"Good news: my two brothers talked me round to granting Zhu Shuzhen her mutual divorce—I was actually ready to cut her loose! Bad news: seeing YOUR smug face just killed that! Divorce is off forever—enjoy waiting till you rot! Haha...!" Snatching a cup, he strutted grinning back to his own table, poured full, raised it high, and hollered: "Cheers, Qiao Wenxian!" Down the hatch, back to eating!
Around the ground floor, nervous patrons poised to bolt relaxed back into their meals as peace returned.
#CH 43
#TITLE: Chapter 43: Helping a Waiter and Watching the Show
Ignoring Lin Jue, Qiao Wenxian fumed inwardly—what on earth had possessed Prefect Zhu to marry his brilliant daughter to such a crude, brutal bully?! His heart ached for Zhu Shuzhen.
Worse, Lin Jue's "bad news" stabbed him with guilt—had his return accidentally wrecked her divorce?!
Rising once more, he walked straight to Lin Jue's table, expression grave and steady: "Lin Jue—what will it take for you to set Youqi Jushi free?"
Startled a split second, Lin Jue barked a cold laugh: "Thought half a word with me was too much—why crawl over here talking?"
Qiao Wenxian stood firm: "What will it take to set Shuzhen free?"
Slamming down his chopsticks, Lin Jue snarled: "Who gave you leave to call her Shuzhen?! Hmph—since you begged for an answer, listen close! Drop on your knees right here before every diner, kowtow three loud times, call me 'Grandpa Lin' three times, and confess aloud you're a two-faced hypocrite lusting after another man's wife—do that, and I'll grant Zhu Shuzhen her mutual divorce!"
Every head across the dining hall snapped round at Lin Jue's roar.
Smug yet secretly sweating—what if the fool actually knelt and forced him to free her?!—Lin Jue stared hard.
Frozen in place, Qiao Wenxian wrestled fiercely inside: kneel, and both pride and his spotless literary name shattered forever! Yet refuse, and how would Shuzhen win freedom? If Shuzhen learned he sacrificed his dignity for her liberty, surely she'd run straight into his arms! Pride and fame were hollow smoke beside Shuzhen!
Mind made up, Qiao Wenxian began bending his knees...
Lin Jue's eyes popped wide in panic.
Inches before his knees hit floorboards, strong hands caught Qiao Wenxian's arms—Shen Jianchen whispering sharp in his ear: "Kneel now, and you prove every dirty accusation he hurled—you'll shred Youqi Jushi's reputation right alongside yours!"
Jolted wide awake, Qiao Wenxian realized his desperate love had blinded him—thank goodness Prince Yi caught him before wrecking Shuzhen's honor!
Exhaling silent relief that he hadn't knelt, Lin Jue sneered: "Then forget me ever letting her go!"
Sure Wenyuan wouldn't do anything foolish now, Shen Jianchen let go and faced Lin Jue cool: "Training Officer—Young Master Qiao here is imperial kin of noble lineage, an honored guest of His Majesty in Bianliang! Skipping proper courtesy is bad enough, yet you bully him in public with degrading demands and drag both his name and Youqi Jushi's through the mud! Youqi Jushi is your own wife—what do you gain smearing her? Truly a tragedy she married you!"
"Imperial kin" triggered an instant alarm in Lin Jue's head! After Zhu Shuzhen left for the countryside, anxious Prefect Zhu might blame him, Lin Jue had called on his father-in-law to say Shuzhen just went out to cool off for a month after the failed divorce.
Not only hadn't Zhu Xiyan blamed him—telling him patience would bring her round—he had dropped big news: the court was dispatching a Military Commissioner to Lin'an, quite possibly an imperial prince!
Studying Shen Jianchen now—commanding bearing, effortless authority, never seen in Lin'an before, arriving straight from Bianliang alongside Qiao Wenxian! Could this guy be that imperial prince?!
Lin Jue's glare melted straight into a smooth smile: "Might Young Master hail from Bianliang?"
Startled by the sudden flip, Shen Jianchen answered flat: "Suppose I do?"
Nine out of ten an imperial prince—play safe! Lin Jue laughed hearty: "Well spoken, Young Master! How could I ever offend imperial kin? Just pulling Young Master Qiao's leg—no hard feelings, Young Master Qiao! To apologize, your entire table is on my tab!"
Spotting the bully footing the bill, Bai Feng jumped right in: "Generous as you are, Nan Shuang and I won't be shy—another bowl of Dongpo pork and a plate of Longjing Shrimp!"
Nan Shuang piled right on: "Add a plate of Hangzhou Chrysanthemum Shredded Chicken!"
"Quiet, you two—depends what the two gentlemen say!" Lin Jue hedged.
"Their word is our word—put it on your tab!" Glowing with sweet satisfaction, Qiao Wenxian returned to his seat beside Prince Yi and resumed eating.
Gritting his teeth yet terrified of offending a possible prince, Lin Jue waved at a waiter: "Bring everything they ordered!" Then he cupped his hands politely toward Shen Jianchen.
Ignoring him, Shen Jianchen murmured low to Wenyuan: "Bet he guessed my identity—why else flip overnight?"
"Good if he did—saved me more public grief."
"Wait—your family IS imperial kin, he's just a local Training Officer! Why'd he treat you like dirt?"
"Imperial kin on paper! My sister Consort Qiao passed ages ago, and you stayed sickly and unfavored—who in Lin'an feared the Qiao house?"
"Makes sense."
Fresh platters landed before Bai Feng and Nan Shuang, who attacked them anew—Juxiang Tower's cooking was divine, just skimpy portions! Round two hit the spot!
Calm settled back over the dining room as satisfied diners left and new guests filtered in, folks drifting up and down from the second-floor rooms.
Glancing over, Shen Jianchen saw Lin Jue eating quietly now, done picking fights.
Beside him, Qiao Wenxian ate distracted, still brooding over Zhu Shuzhen's divorce.
Suddenly a waiter stumbled down the stairs pale and frantic; spotting Qiao Wenxian, he dropped to his knees weeping: "Young Master Qiao! Your word carries weight—please save me! Beg the boss not to fire me! Got a wife, kids, and old mother depending on me—lose this job and we starve! Sob...!"
"Why's the boss firing you?" Qiao Wenxian asked at once.
"One of Young Master Chen's thugs harassed a respectable girl outside and I spoke up two words! Today he came upstairs with Young Master Chen, bumped my tray on purpose dumping dishes on the floor—Young Master Chen chewed out the boss for clumsy service and ordered me fired! Please speak up for me, Young Master Qiao—I can't lose this work! Sob...!"
Hurrying down behind him, the restaurant manager sighed: "Just go quietly, lad—keep you on and Young Master Chen will smash Juxiang Tower flat! Tell you what, I'll pay you three extra months' wages!"
Standing up, Qiao Wenxian offered: "Hold on, Manager—let me go talk to Young Master Chen! He should give me face."
Puzzled and wary, Shen Jianchen murmured: "Sounds like this Chen guy is nastier than Lin Jue—how do you know he'll give you face?"
With a cryptic smile and no explanation, Qiao Wenxian headed upstairs.
"Playing mysterious on me!" Shen Jianchen muttered.
Over at his table, Lin Jue sneered: "Overestimating himself!" Going right back to food and wine, whenever he caught Shen Jianchen glancing his way he raised his cup in a polite toast.
Shen Jianchen lifted his tea bowl in return and drained it clean.
Down on the floor the waiter stayed kneeling while the manager pulled up a chair waiting.
Less than a quarter hour later, Qiao Wenxian strolled back down the stairs: "All sorted, Manager—Young Master Chen dropped it; the waiter keeps his job."
Jumping up, the manager beamed: "Nobody carries pull like Young Master Qiao! Much obliged!"
Spinning toward Qiao Wenxian, the waiter banged his forehead hard on the floor: "Thank you, Young Master Qiao! Thank you!"
"Quit kowtowing—get back to work!" Qiao Wenxian smiled.
Scrambling up amid endless blessings, the waiter hustled back to serving tables.
Genuine gratitude warmed the manager's face: "Hardworking kid, hated letting him go—thanks to you, Young Master Qiao! Let me send one more dish to your table on the house!"
Full already, Qiao Wenxian looked to Prince Yi: "Want anything else, Young Master Shen?"
Shen Jianchen shook his head.
Bai Feng piped right up: "Don't waste the manager's kindness, Young Master—let's get one more!"
Shen Jianchen nodded toward the manager: "Give the extra dish to him."
"What would you like, young brother?" the manager chuckled.
Without blinking: "Another bowl of Dongpo pork!"
Calling toward the kitchen: "One Dongpo pork for the lad!"
Once Qiao Wenxian sat back down, Shen Jianchen pressed: "Spill the secret now!"
"Nothing dramatic—when Young Master Chen was little, he fell in a river. Passing by, I hauled him out with everything I had! He's remembered that debt ever since, stays polite to me, and gives me face."
"How old were you pulling him out?"
"Eight—Chen Haoyu was five."
"Little hero!" Shen Jianchen grinned.
Watching Qiao Wenxian effortlessly rescue the waiter only soured Lin Jue more: Big deal! Cozy with scum like Chen Haoyu just proves birds of a feather!
Right on cue, Chen Haoyu sauntered down from the second floor flanked by two wealthy cronies and three thugs.
Flushed from feasting, Chen Haoyu spotted Qiao Wenxian and strolled over: "We're officially even now, Qiao Wenxian! Debt paid in full—never bring up that old favor again, because next time you get zero face!"
Cool and composed, Qiao Wenxian answered: "Rest easy—I'll never mention childhood favors again. Slate wiped clean."
Turning to leave, Chen Haoyu locked eyes straight onto Lin Jue—and a vicious idea sparked: Hmph! Humiliated twice by your tigress of a wife with nowhere to vent—walk right into my lap, might as well rub salt in YOUR pride to save mine!
Sauntering over to Lin Jue's table, he drawled: "Gotta admire your big heart, Training Officer Lin! Wife living out in the countryside alone and you're chilling over fine lunch at Juxiang Tower?"
Burning inside—everybody in town knows she ran off to the country, wait till she gets back!—Lin Jue kept cool and rose to leave: "Enjoy yourself, Young Master Chen—got duties calling, farewell."
Instantly Chen Haoyu's three thugs stepped up barring Lin Jue's exit. Halting, Lin Jue snapped: "Why block my way, Young Master Chen? I've never crossed you."
"Haven't finished talking—walking off mid-sentence is mighty rude!"
Swallowing irritation, Lin Jue stood stiff: "Speak, Young Master Chen."
Chen Haoyu grinned wicked: "Know WHERE Zhu Shuzhen is staying out in the country? Haha—right inside MY bamboo grove! Know what that means? Means she'd rather camp in my grove than step back into Lin Manor—clearly she's sweet on me! Dated endless young virgins before, never tried a married woman—might just break my fast on Zhu Shuzhen..."
Before Lin Jue even moved, Qiao Wenxian shot half out of his chair ready to swing—only for Shen Jianchen to hold him down: "He's baiting Lin Jue—wait and watch."
"He's trashing Shuzhen's honor in public! How can I sit watching?!"
"Her own husband hasn't swung yet! You jump in now and you don't clear her name—you feed gossip! Sit tight and watch the show!"
Seeing sense in Prince Yi's warning, Qiao Wenxian forced his temper down.
Just then the waiter dropped the fresh bowl of Dongpo pork on the side table and bolted fast sensing trouble.
Long as their masters stayed safe, nothing beat hot Dongpo pork—Bai Feng and Nan Shuang dove straight back in like bottomless pits!
Stung raw by Chen Haoyu's filth—first worrying she met Qiao Wenxian out there, now Chen Haoyu?! Shameless woman!—yet outnumbered three to one, Lin Jue tried pushing past only for the thugs to close tighter.
"Don't run yet—best part's coming!" Chen Haoyu cackled. "Heard Zhu Shuzhen's demanding divorce—know why? Hehe... because she wants to marry ME! Hurry up and cut her loose so I can bring her home!"
Zhus and Chens were in-laws, visiting back and forth constantly—what if they really HAD carried on behind his back?! Blind rage snapped Lin Jue's leash; clenching his fist, he swung straight at Chen Haoyu's face!
#CH 44
#TITLE: Chapter 44: Storytelling in Goulan Wasi
Chen Haoyu ducked clean under Lin Jue's punch, and all three thugs piled straight onto Lin Jue, who scrambled to fend them off.
Fearing flying fists, ground-floor diners scrambled out the door; waiters ducked behind the counter peeking at the brawl while the manager winced praying his tables survived.
Three against one, Lin Jue sagged fast toward defeat.
Watching from his seat, Shen Jianchen frowned—like him or not, Lin Jue was an imperial military officer! Letting local thugs beat an officer flat in public looked awful.
Too green in Shen Fist yet for Bai Feng to handle three bruisers together, Shen Jianchen stepped in himself.
Vaulting beside Lin Jue in one smooth leap, he dropped all three thugs in three crisp blows and held up calm hands: "Talk things out like gentlemen—why swing fists?"
That sealed Lin Jue's certainty—definitely an imperial prince! Otherwise why step into another man's brawl alongside Qiao Wenxian instead of laughing? Protecting an imperial officer's face!
Reading Shen Jianchen's effortless power, Chen Haoyu knew picking a fight with him meant getting flattened—besides, he'd already stung Lin Jue and trashed Zhu Shuzhen's name, mission accomplished! Waving his three groaning thugs back, he sneered:
"Not worth dirtying my hands on you, Lin Jue! One parting tip: cast Zhu Shuzhen out fast! She's a straight-up tigress—keep her around and she'll bite your head off! Haha...!" Roaring with laughter, he swaggered out with his crew.
Lin Jue cupped his hands grateful: "Thank you for stepping in, Young Master."
"Helped you so you stop misunderstanding Youqi Jushi and Wenyuan," Shen Jianchen murmured near his ear. "Wenyuan admires your wife, sure—yet everything between them stays spotless. Even if you don't trust Wenyuan, trust your own wife! Without basic trust, a marriage is already dead."
Hardly convinced yet polite before a presumed prince, Lin Jue bowed: "Wise counsel, Young Master—much obliged! Let me host a banquet at Lin Manor someday to repay your help."
"No need—putting today's meal on your tab squares us fine."
"Then Lin Jue takes his leave." Striding out of Juxiang Tower, Lin Jue walked home torn in knots: cast her out? Grant mutual divorce? Or keep dragging her along?
Finishing every last scrap on the table, Bai Feng and Nan Shuang sat back patting full bellies.
Qiao Wenxian laughed shaking his head: "All you two ever do is eat, eat, eat!"
"Only thanks to Master's big heart we get feasts like this!" Nan Shuang grinned.
Rising, Shen Jianchen suggested: "Still want to check out Goulan Wasi—everyone game?"
All agreed; stepping out of Juxiang Tower, Qiao Wenxian led them toward nearby Qiaotou Wa.
Bright banners and vibrant crowds packed the entertainment district—storytelling, puppetry, shadow plays, acrobatics, song-and-dance, and opera performing inside separate Goulan theaters while peddlers hawked trinkets and snacks all around.
Flipping Bai Feng one tael of silver, Shen Jianchen said: "Pick whichever Goulan theater you two want—Wenyuan and I are hitting storytelling."
Catching the coin—"Thanks, Young Master!"—Bai Feng dragged Nan Shuang straight into a lively song-and-dance theater brimming with beauties.
Used to hitting Goulan Wasi with scholar buddies for drinks, poetry, and occasional tales, Qiao Wenxian gladly led the prince into a storytelling hall.
Inside, a storyteller sat animated atop a low stage behind a wooden table holding a folding fan, gray teapot, and tea bowl.
Packed rows of listeners clustered around small tables munching candied preserves and sipping tea as waiters darted through refilling pots; spotting newcomers, one called: "Two gentlemen—right this way!"
Prime front seats all taken, they settled at a back table.
Qiao Wenxian ordered two plates of candied preserves and a pot of tea, leaning in to listen.
The storyteller's voice rang across the hall:
"Now Cui Ning returned home in low spirits, stepped into his chamber, and saw his wife seated upon the bed. Cui Ning cried, 'Mercy, sister—spare my life!' Xiuxiu replied, 'Because of you, the Prince had me beaten to death and buried in the rear garden! I hated Officer Guo for his loose tongue—today my vengeance is done, the Prince had him flogged fifty strokes across the back! Now all know I am a ghost and I can linger no longer.' Speaking thus, she rose and gripped Cui Ning with both hands; one shriek, and he collapsed limp to the floor. When neighbors rushed in to see: both pulses stilled and sunk away, one soul gone down to yellow earth! Cui Ning too was dragged off to join her parents—four ghosts together..." (Excerpt from "The Jade Guanyin") [TN: Translator's rendering]
Clack! The storyteller struck his gavel block on the table, closing the tale, and stepped down off the stage.
"Bravo!" thundered from the crowd.
Catching only the closing lines, Shen Jianchen blinked baffled: "What was that even about? Lost me completely!"
"Bright beauty Xiuxiu fell in love with jade carver Cui Ning, got killed by a cruel prince, and came back to make them ghost spouses in the underworld," Qiao Wenxian sighed. "How many devoted lovers denied in life can only cling to death or the next life—tragic, truly tragic!"
Clearly Wenyuan's own ache for Zhu Shuzhen colored his sigh. Unconcerned with Wenyuan's heartbreak, Shen Jianchen focused sharp—storytelling tied straight to the "mouth"! Maybe fate waited right here!
A second storyteller mounted the stage gripping a purple clay teapot, took a swig right off the spout, set it down, snapped open his folding fan, and bantered with the crowd: "Folks, just scored a brand-new huaben script—fresh off the brush! Care to hear something new?"
Voices shouted back: "Heard those dusty classics a hundred times—give us fresh!"
"Yeah, tell the new one!" others cheered.
"You asked for it—listen close!" Snapping his fan shut, the storyteller launched smooth into the tale:
"When Goddess Nüwa mended the shattered heavens, a thunderbolt startled her into a shiver, shaking loose a wildflower tucked behind her ear! Carried a thousand miles on the wind, it drifted straight into the abode of the Flower Deity. Just then the Flower Deity was full-term with child, laboring in agony unable to deliver—when wind blew Nüwa's wildflower straight onto her belly and vanished! Instant baby wails rang out as the Flower Deity gave birth to a baby girl! 'Born from a nameless crimson wildflower radiating enchanting scent,' the Flower Deity declared, 'from this day on her name shall be Hongxiang...'"
Wait—WAIT A SECOND! That plot rang like thunder in Shen Jianchen's skull! Racking his memory, realization hit like lightning: THAT WAS ONE OF QIANLI XUN'S WEB NOVELS!! Heart slamming against his ribs, he held his breath straining to confirm!
The storyteller rolled on:
"The Flower Deity's realm was called Hundred-Blossom Palace. Pampered by flower fairies, Hongxiang grew into a radiant beauty—yet mischievous to the bone, forever sneaking out of the palace to play! One day she slipped away unseen and landed upon Cinnabar Cave Mountain—a wonderland sliced by a shimmering golden river. While admiring the peaks, a dazzling five-colored phoenix alighted on a boulder behind her and transformed into a breathtaking young man..."
Shen Jianchen's fingers trembled around his tea bowl as he whispered aloud: "Named Xuanying."
Right on cue from the stage: "The young man's name was Xuanying..."
Qiao Wenxian whipped his head round in shock: "First time anyone's ever heard this tale—how on earth did YOU know?!"
Shen Jianchen didn't hear a word.
Staring at Prince Yi's frozen face—eyes locked unblinking onto the storyteller, hand setting down his tea bowl with visibly shaking fingers—Qiao Wenxian asked uneasy: "What's wrong with you?"
Still mute, Shen Jianchen rose slowly to his feet, eyes glued to the stage.
Wait—had Qianli Xun transmigrated INTO THAT STORYTELLER?! Taking stiff steps toward the stage, he had zero clue how the man would react if he called "Qianli Xun"!
Thinking she had crossed into a short, slightly chubby middle-aged man hit him with a wave of deflation—yet in the next heartbeat he swallowed it: fine, romance off the table, they could still be best friends for life! Blinking back hot tears, he kept closing in on the stage...
Watching bewildered from the table, Qiao Wenxian wondered: What's gotten into Prince Yi?!
Up on stage the storyteller carried on:
"Xuanying asked, 'Who are you, and what brings you to Cinnabar Cave Mountain?' Turning around, Hongxiang stole his breath away—never had he seen such beauty on the mountain! Hongxiang tossed the question back: 'Who are you, and why do you live here?' Xuanying answered, 'My name is Xuanying, and Cinnabar Cave Mountain is my home...'"
Seeing the young gentleman stride straight up, the storyteller figured he just wanted a closer seat—until Shen Jianchen choked out: "Qianli Xun!" Halting mid-sentence at the raw emotion on the stranger's face, the storyteller blinked: "You know Qianli Xun, young master?"
"I'm Shen Jianchen—I finally found you!" One hot tear broke free and slid down Shen Jianchen's cheek.
Utterly perplexed, the storyteller shook his head: "Never heard of Shen Jianchen—I know the name Qianli Xun alright, though I've never met the man myself!"
Relief rushed over Shen Jianchen so hard he almost laughed at his own runaway meltdown! Cupping his hands urgent, he pleaded: "If you know Qianli Xun, sir—tell me where she is!"
"No idea where he lives! Only know he's a talented scholar who supplies me huaben scripts—every time, his household servant brings the new pages over."
Jeers broke out from the crowd: "Hey! Keep telling the story—we're waiting!" "Right—paid good silver to hear a tale, not chat!"
"Step aside till I finish my set, young master—we'll talk after," the storyteller urged.
Turning back to the room, he picked the story right back up.
Returning to his seat, Shen Jianchen couldn't focus on another word, willing the clock to fly so he could pump the storyteller for leads.
Studying Prince Yi with open curiosity, Qiao Wenxian asked: "Who is Qianli Xun?"
"A friend."
"Another friend from that 'other world'?"
Shen Jianchen nodded.
Dropping further questions—whatever Prince Yi claimed happened after death sounded like fever dreams to Wenyuan, though that martial skill stayed undeniably uncanny—he sipped his tea quiet.
The instant the storyteller stepped down from the stage, Shen Jianchen darted straight over: "Sir—when does Qianli Xun's servant bring the next manuscript?"
Pondering a beat, the storyteller shrugged: "Never fixed—sometimes two or three days, sometimes five or six."
"Where do you live?"
"Why ask that?"
"Want to post my man outside your house waiting for him."
"No way, no way!" The storyteller waved hard.
Shen Jianchen pressed two taels of silver straight into his palm: "For your trouble—and more reward once I find Qianli Xun!"
Pocketing the silver with an instant grin, the storyteller spilled: "West Third Alley—very first house inside the lane!"
"Thank you, sir!" Joy sparked bright—then dimmed a shade as the storyteller's wording sank back in: a talented scholar... So Qianli Xun really DID transmigrate into a man after all!
Clapping his shoulder from behind, Qiao Wenxian asked: "Lost in space—ready to head back?"
With a solid trail finally in hand, sightseeing was done: "Long day, tired—let's go home."
Rounding up Bai Feng and Nan Shuang, they hired a local carriage through busy markets out to the city gate, swapped into their own rig, and rolled back toward Youlin Retreat.
Inside the carriage, Shen Jianchen issued crisp orders to Bai Feng: starting tomorrow morning, camp outside the first house inside West Third Alley until the manuscript courier showed up—and bring that courier straight back to him in person!
Clueless what his master was chasing yet eager as ever, Bai Feng saluted yes on the spot.
#CH 45
#TITLE: Chapter 45: Scholars and Ladies at Zuiyun Tower
Night fell clear and calm, a sea of stars framing a luminous half-moon.
With a real lead on Qianli Xun at last, warm comfort eased Shen Jianchen's chest. Peering out the carriage window, he caught the silhouette of the Divine Willow in the distance, silver moonlight turning its sweeping fronds slender and graceful.
Quietly in his mind drifted the line: "The moon rises above the willow tips; our tryst is set past dusk." [TN: Translator's rendering] Romance stirred—only to catch on the bitter worry that the soul stirring it might now wear a male scholar's body! Relief and melancholy tangled together.
As the carriage rolled beneath Youlin's canopy hiding the willow from view, Shen Jianchen pulled his gaze inward and rode silent across from Wenyuan.
Tactful, Wenyuan rested with shut eyes.
Stepping down at Youlin Retreat, Butler Li hurried out to greet them: "Home at last, Prince Yi and Young Master—Princess Jiahe has been asking after you nonstop!"
Qiao Wenxian grinned teasing: "Speak accurate, Butler Li—Princess Jiahe asks after Prince Yi alone! Poor bachelor me, nothing like Prince Yi having a beauty waiting everywhere he turns!"
Butler Li chuckled: "High time you thought of marriage too, Young Master! Lady Miao seems a fine match—heard you got back from Bianliang and called in person today! Missed you, left saying she'll visit another day."
"Who's Lady Miao? Come clean!" Shen Jianchen teased.
Dodging fast, Qiao Wenxian nudged him: "Better hurry see Princess Jiahe and cure her lovesickness!"
Bantering, they strolled inside the grounds.
Butler Li remembered one more message: "Oh—Young Master Dongfang sent word inviting you to Zuiyun Tower tomorrow to appreciate lyrics—new work by Youqi Jushi, begs you not to miss it!"
"Got it—go tend your work, Butler Li, we know our way from here."
Bowing, Butler Li withdrew.
Turning to Prince Yi, Qiao Wenxian invited: "Care to tag along to tomorrow's poetry salon?"
Wait—poetry salon meant scholars! If Qianli Xun had transmigrated into a male scholar, maybe she'd show up there!
"Sure—count me in!"
Walking together a bit further, they split paths toward their courtyards.
Bai Feng trailed behind Prince Yi toward Princess Jiahe's rear garden, keeping both hands tucked suspiciously behind his back the whole walk.
Left alone at the retreat all day, Qiao Mengrong had tasted the sharpest longing—and dread—of her life.
Out roaming town, had Brother slipped into Goulan Wasi? Word was those theaters teemed with dazzling songstresses, dancers, and musicians—what if he got cozy with them?
Around the old Zhao Pu, she had felt rock-solid confidence; around this new healthy brother, insecurity gnawed nonstop.
Darkness fallen and still no sign of him—had he wandered into pleasure quarters? Lin'an beauties were renowned across the land; what if tender arms made him forget his little sister?
Unable to bear another second, she stepped out to send Butler Li searching town—
And froze right past her doorway seeing Brother standing in her courtyard! Overwhelmed by relief and longing, she threw herself straight into his arms!
"Gone all day long, Brother—I worried myself sick! From now on, wherever you go, you HAVE to bring me!"
Coming by simply to check in so she wouldn't fret—after all her selfless devotion to Zhao Pu—he hadn't braced for another sudden embrace. Instead of joy, heavier guilt weighed his shoulders; Zhao Pu's love felt like an iron yoke he couldn't shake.
Still keeping his arms down, he promised gentle: "Alright—wherever I go next, you come along. Speaking of which, Wenyuan invited me to a poetry salon tomorrow—come with us."
Like sunshine breaking storm clouds, her face lit up! Letting go with a bright grin: "I'll go with you tomorrow, Brother—won't embarrass you!"
At a total loss what to say next, Shen Jianchen stood stiff and awkward.
Right then Bai Feng stepped up before Qiao Mengrong and whipped a paper parcel from behind his back: "Princess! His Highness bought you chestnut cakes while out in town—super tasty, tested 'em myself!"
Taking the parcel, Qiao Mengrong beamed head to toe: "Thank you for thinking of me, Brother! Chestnut cakes from you are guaranteed the sweetest in the whole world!"
Having spent all day chasing Qianli Xun without one spare thought for Mengrong, Shen Jianchen realized Bai Feng had bought them on his own—oh well, chalk it up as doing Zhao Pu's duty!
"Getting late, Mengrong—get some rest."
"Rest well too, Brother."
Watching his departing back, she passed the cakes to Xilan: "Plate some up—I want a few right now!"
Taking the package, Xilan ducked indoors.
Gazing at the empty spot where he had stood, a wistful shadow returned: hugged him tight again, still not a single arm wrapped back around her... when would he ever pull her into his chest on his own again?
Back in the main courtyard, Shen Jianchen snapped brisk: "Start drilling boxing! One hour before bed!"
Bai Feng groaned: "Aw, Your Highness—just got to Lin'an, can't we take a couple nights off?!"
"No!" Shen Jianchen put on a stern face.
"Not even one night off for buying Princess Jiahe chestnut cakes in your name?!"
"No!"
Stuck under a strict master, Bai Feng sighed and started throwing forms in the yard.
Heading inside to turn in, Shen Jianchen dozed off—waking nearly two hours later to steady thuds and whooshes still echoing outside! Getting into the groove, the kid had blown straight past bedtime!
Stepping into the yard: "Still going? Wrap it up and hit the sack!"
Halting mid-stance, Bai Feng blinked: "Hey, Your Highness—how come the longer I drill, the lighter I feel? Not even tired!"
"Means you've caught the inner rhythm of Shen Fist—officially crossed the threshold! You'll advance twice as fast from here."
Gratitude shone in Bai Feng's eyes: "You're awesome, Your Highness!"
"Where'd that come from?"
"Always felt it! Gave me a home, teach me real martial arts, feed me banquets, hand me silver—best prince on earth! So I want YOU happy—happy right alongside Princess Jiahe!"
"So THAT'S why you bought her chestnut cakes?"
Bai Feng nodded earnest: "Your Highness stays busy and slips up—I look out for Princess Jiahe for you!"
Shen Jianchen couldn't bring himself to praise him—were he in love with Mengrong, brilliant wingman; feeling zero romance for her, Bai Feng's sweet moves only dug his guilt deeper!
"Keep your head on your boxing and stop playing matchmaker—especially between Princess Jiahe and me! And don't forget tomorrow's job: West Third Alley!"
"Locked in, Your Highness!"
Tossing him a pouch of silver: "Five taels inside—catch!"
Catching it with a wide grin: "Best trait about Your Highness is deep pockets—food, roof, kung fu, AND silver!"
"Enough lip—go sleep!"
...
Perched in a scenic spot outside Lin'an's walls, Zuiyun Tower took a full hour by carriage to reach.
Keeping his word, Shen Jianchen brought Qiao Mengrong along. Stepping down to behold a broad river sweeping past rolling green hills across the far bank, Qiao Mengrong sighed in wonder at Lin'an's beauty.
Scanning around—aside from river and hills, nothing but open countryside! Zuiyun Tower rose lone by the riverbank looking totally out of place; clearly local peasants couldn't afford dining here—how on earth did it stay open?
"How's business way out here, Wenyuan?"
"Look around—dead empty!"
"Then why build it here?!"
"No clue—just know it belongs to the Chen family, richest house in Lin'an, couldn't care less if one tavern loses coin! Rumor says they built the entire tower simply because little Chen Haoyu liked the view here as a kid!"
"Same Chen Haoyu we ran into yesterday?"
"The one and only!"
No use asking further—billionaire whims baffled regular minds! If the Chens didn't care about profits, why sweat it?
Following Wenyuan inside Zuiyun Tower revealed sheer opulence: grand rosewood table centering the entry hall ringed with exquisite bonsai; fine mountain-and-bird scrolls covering the walls bringing nature indoors; rear banquet hall lined with crimson-silk-draped tables gleaming with fine porcelain...
A middle-aged man stepped up smooth: "Here for Young Master Dongfang's poetry salon, gentlemen?"
"Yes," Qiao Wenxian confirmed.
Noticing the man's silk robes rivaled Wenyuan's and his own in quality—a Chen relative?—Shen Jianchen studied him curious.
"I am chief steward of Zuiyun Tower. Young Master Dongfang's gathering is upstairs—follow me."
Odd—unlike busy inns teeming with waiters yesterday, not a single waiter stood in sight!
Upstairs opened onto a bright landing bordered by closed private rooms.
Pushing open one door, the steward announced: "Young Master Dongfang—Young Master Qiao and guests have arrived!"
Stepping aside to leave, the steward withdrew as Qiao Wenxian entered first, followed by Shen Jianchen, Qiao Mengrong with Xilan, and Nan Shuang bringing up the rear—far quieter without Bai Feng to banter with.
Dongfang Chenze jumped up and wrapped Qiao Wenxian in a big hug: "Missed you to pieces, Brother Qiao! Stay home for good now—salons lost half their spark without you!"
Hugging him tight back: "Missed you too, Brother Dongfang—all of you!"
Cao Ban and An Mingxuan stepped right up for warm hugs too once Dongfang let go.
Just as Qiao Wenxian released Cao Ban, a soft, musical woman's voice spoke:
"Delighted to discuss verse alongside Young Master Qiao once more."
Turning, Qiao Wenxian met Miao Zhiyu executing an elegant curtsy.
Returning a courteous bow: "Long time no see, Lady Miao—Wenyuan pays respects."
Hiding a racing pulse beneath ladylike poise, Miao Zhiyu glowed inside—away almost a year, back at last! Now salons meant hearing his voice and brilliant insights again!
Spotting the newcomers behind Wenyuan, Dongfang Chenze asked: "Introduce your friends, Brother Qiao!"
Keeping Prince Yi's rank under wraps as requested, Qiao Wenxian introduced them smooth: "Young Master Shen, a friend I made in Bianliang—and Miss Qiao, a distant cousin of mine."
Cupping hands polite, Shen Jianchen greeted them: "Hearing Wenyuan had a salon, Mengrong and I got curious and tagged along—hope we aren't intruding."
"Intruding? Honored to have you! Quit standing—everyone take a seat!"
Sitting beside Qiao Wenxian, Shen Jianchen scanned the three unfamiliar scholars—hunting any sign of Qianli Xun!
Qiao Mengrong settled next to Miao Zhiyu; trading courteous smiles, one girl kept adoring eyes anchored on her brother while the other stole shy peeks at Wenyuan!
Qiao Wenxian asked curious: "Why pick way out here for today's salon, Brother Dongfang? Lovely view, sure, zero atmosphere!"
"Between friends, no shame admitting it: short on cash!" Dongfang Chenze laughed.
"Short on cash I get—how's that explain Zuiyun Tower?"
"Ghostwrote a poem for Chen Haoyu so he passed his dad's test! Thrilled, he lent me Zuiyun Tower free for the day—unlimited vintage wine, chef on standby cooking anything we order! Pinching pennies, how could I pass up free luxury?!"
Cao Ban chimed in: "Turns out this whole tower runs on just one steward and one chef—steward says normally nobody sets foot here except Young Master Chen!"
An Mingxuan grinned: "Got here early and ordered signature dishes—chef knew every single one! Steward says the Chens poached him straight from Bianliang's Fan Tower—master cook who took the gig for easy hours and massive pay!"
Right then the steward entered bearing two silver wine pots on a tray: "Young Master Chen ordered top hospitality—two pots of fine vintage, light sweet, zero burn, hint of pomegranate, ideal for both ladies too!"
Setting the tray down, he poured full cups all around.
Once filled, Dongfang Chenze waved: "Withdraw for now, Steward—we'll call when needed."
"Yes, sir—soon as dishes are ready, the chef and I will bring everything up." Setting the pots down, he backed out with a bow.
#CH 46
#TITLE: Chapter 46: Listening in Silence, Appraising a Masterwork
Seeing zero trace of Qianli Xun among the three scholars, Shen Jianchen let his eyes roam the room's decor.
Dominating one wall was a grand circular window carved in openwork like a spreading tree, framing crisp river and mountain views beyond.
Beside the window sat an empty head seat—among equal friends nobody pulled rank, so everyone took side seats along both flanks.
Against the opposite wall stood a long brocade-draped console lined with fine porcelain vases and miniature bonsai beneath hanging calligraphy scrolls lending quiet elegance.
Thinking back to yesterday's run-in with Chen Haoyu—handsome shell over zero brains—decorating with lofty paintings felt comically phony! A sardonic smirk tugged Shen Jianchen's mouth.
While he observed the room, Qiao Mengrong observed him.
First time seeing Brother mingle with friends beyond Wenyuan, his quiet composure captivated her—even that brief curve of his lip, plainly amused rather than warm.
What went on inside his head? Impossible to guess.
One sweet comfort: seated right beside her was Miao Zhiyu, a textbook Jiangnan beauty even Mengrong admired—yet Brother didn't spare her a single glance! Either immune to stray eyes, or carrying Mengrong alone in his heart!
A soft, pleased smile lit Qiao Mengrong's face.
Qiao Wenxian opened the discussion: "Hear we're reading Youqi Jushi's work today—her newest?"
"Brand-new!" Dongfang Chenze confirmed. "Wanted to invite her too, pity she's off staying in the countryside—rumor says cooling off after her divorce fell through."
Qiao Wenxian sighed bitter: "One step too late—could've stood by her!"
Miao Zhiyu echoed with a sigh: "Men take wives and concubines at will, yet once a woman marries wrong, starting over feels impossible! Long as the Training Officer refuses, Youqi Jushi has zero exit."
Cao Ban protested quick: "Known us years, Lady Miao—don't you know we live by 'seeking one devoted heart, never parting till hair turns white'? [TN: Translator's rendering]"
"True—you gentlemen differ from the rest," Miao Zhiyu smiled, glancing sideways toward Qiao Wenxian. "As Youqi Jushi's true confidant, Young Master Qiao will surely uncover rare depths for us today!"
"With your own brilliance and years of sisterhood with Youqi Jushi, Lady Miao, you know her soul far deeper than Wenyuan—eager to hear your reading first!" Qiao Wenxian deflected polite.
An Mingxuan groaned playful: "Quit trading bows, you two! Bring out Youqi Jushi's gem already, Brother Dongfang—we're dying to see it!"
Teasing them, Dongfang Chenze grinned: "Patience—let me tell you how I scored it first!"
"Spill it already!" Cao Ban urged.
"Strolling through Donghe Wa the other day, I ran into a literary gathering admiring a brand-new lyric by Youqi Jushi—brought by Training Officer Lin's own Second Uncle, Lin Zixiao! Afterward I treated Lin Zixiao to drinks; knowing I'm friends with her, he opened up! Turns out on her divorce day Prefect Zhu never showed—only the Zhu clan's century-old Old Ancestor appeared, moved straight out of seclusion by this very lyric! Moved too, Lin Zixiao backed the divorce, so Youqi Jushi gifted him the scroll! Begged him for the original, wouldn't budge—had to borrow ink and copy it word for word!"
Heart aching harder, Qiao Wenxian sighed: "With Prefect Zhu opposing it, breaking free of Lin Jue gets ten times harder! Imagine how crushed and alone she felt when not one elder stepped up—desperate enough to drag out an Old Ancestor who never leaves her shrine!"
Hearing them hash over Zhu Shuzhen again left Shen Jianchen yawning inside—aside from Wenyuan's crush, zero interest! His mind drifted to Bai Feng staking out West Third Alley today—wonder if the courier turned up?
"Show us the lyric already, Young Master Dongfang!" Miao Zhiyu urged.
"Huayu!" Dongfang Chenze called.
Stepping before the main seat, Huayu unrolled a long scroll; too wide for one pair of hands, Nan Shuang hopped over to hold the far end taut.
Glancing up, Shen Jianchen froze—three bold characters jumped straight off the paper: "The Heartbreak Riddle"! Wait—Zhu Shuzhen?! Suddenly a vivid memory from Qianli Fist Manor slammed straight back into him...
Walking into Qianli Fist Manor's courtyard one sunny day, he had spotted Qianli Xun sitting engrossed in a book—looking nothing like the tough girl calling herself "I, the boss" every sentence!
Standing right before her for a full minute without her noticing, he finally asked: "What book's got you glued?"
Only then did she look up: "What do you want? Can't you see I'm busy?"
"Long holiday coming up—want to road-trip to Tibet with me?"
Flipping her book facedown on the patio table, she stood: "Too slow! Already promised my apprentices a beach trip!"
Pivoting on a dime, he beamed: "Awesome! I'll bring my apprentices too!"
Eyeing his goofy grin, Qianli Xun lectured earnest: "Shen Jianchen, I know you're chasing me—save your breath! Don't want romance, don't want marriage, just want to grow Qianli Fist and live my own life! Quit wasting time on me!"
Dodging her rejection smooth, he pointed at the book cover—"Heartbreak Lyrics" (Duanchang Ci): "Whose book are you reading?"
"You hate poetry anyway—why ask?"
Hated poetry, sure—yet Qianli Xun loved it! Picking up the book and flipping pages like a serious scholar to change the subject, he remarked: "Classical verse—Heartbreak Lyrics, and the page you're on is titled 'The Heartbreak Riddle'! Who wrote something this gloomy?"
"Got eyes—read the cover!"
Flipping to the front: "Ah, Zhu Shuzhen—Song female ci poet! Only ever heard of Li Qingzhao, never Zhu Shuzhen."
"Shows how clueless you are."
Thrilled to keep her chatting: "Tell me about Zhu Shuzhen!"
"Pass—hate playing lute for a cow."
"Then explain 'The Heartbreak Riddle' you were just reading!"
Softening a notch, she took the book back, pulled a tissue from the box, folded it neat over the footnotes, and grinned: "Fine—guess the riddles!"
"Thought it was a poem—why riddles?"
"Hides ten number riddles! Read the first two lines and take a guess!"
Holding the book open with one hand pinning the tissue down, she leaned in.
Leaning right beside her—closer than ever before, breathing in her crisp scent—Shen Jianchen barely focused on two words and drew a total blank!
Laughing, she walked him through the first clues until the pattern clicked and he cracked every remaining number fast, earning a rare grin: "Not totally hopeless!"
Even better, in a high mood she actually agreed to merge their beach trips! Didn't win her heart that week—yet that memory stayed etched forever!
Across the table, Qiao Mengrong watched Brother drop his eyes right after reading the scroll, staring into his wine cup with a tender, secret smile...
What memory made him smile like that?!
A cold ache pinched her chest—ignoring Miao Zhiyu, ignoring HER too, locked inside a private world! Was he picturing another woman?!
