Recalling the fortune-teller's stall—Brother looking for an "old friend"... WAS THAT FRIEND A WOMAN?! Yet Zhao Pu had never known another girl in his life! Could he really not be Prince Yi Zhao Pu?!
Refusing to follow that dark trail, she snapped back as An Mingxuan's exclamation rang out!
Mirroring Lin Zixiao's reaction, An Mingxuan cried: "Genius! Pure genius—weaving character riddles seamless into a ci!"
Cao Ban jumped in: "Every mind here cracks those riddles in seconds—let's skip riddle-guessing and critique the lyric itself!"
Everyone nodded agreement.
"I'll drain a cup to kick off!" Down went Dongfang Chenze's wine as he began: "Written while fighting for mutual divorce, every stroke radiates iron resolve! 'Heartbreak' anchors the theme—never mentions the word once in the body, yet bleeds heartbreak line after line, above all 'from this day on lean no more on a foe'! How dark must her married days be to brand Lin Jue an enemy? Small wonder—since her wedding, she showed up at every salon bruised! Breaks our hearts watching!"
Miao Zhiyu chimed in: "No need to fret quite as much now—at Yongtai Teahouse last time we saw Youqi Jushi knows serious martial arts! Sent Young Master Chen running for his life! Bet she only took Lin Jue's blows before to build grounds to leave—hard to believe we knew her years without guessing she trained in secret!"
Hearing Zhu Shuzhen knew martial arts piqued Shen Jianchen's admiration—kind of curious to meet Wenyuan's idol someday!
Immersed in her pain till now, Qiao Wenxian blinked stunned at Miao Zhiyu's news: "Since when does she know martial arts?!"
Thrilled he addressed her directly, Miao Zhiyu answered bright:
"Hides her depths well! Ask her yourself once she's back from the countryside! Allow Zhiyu to share humble thoughts next."
Draining her pomegranate wine—light, sweet, zero harsh bite—she spoke wistful:
"Woman to woman, her anguish cuts bone-deep. Heart pledged to one love, barred from him, forced to obey parents' orders and matchmakers' words only to land with the wrong man—bitterness and finality blaze between every character! When even a woman of Youqi Jushi's gifts and backbone ends up trapped like this, what hope lies ahead for someone like me?" Her eyes drifted straight onto Qiao Wenxian.
Catching "heart pledged to one love," Qiao Wenxian worried—still hung up on Young Master Xu? He vowed to help break her free AND wipe Xu clean from her heart!
Cao Ban teased playful: "Joining our salons all these years, Lady Miao—if your eye's fallen on anyone here, speak up fast so we march him straight to Miao Manor with a matchmaker!"
Blushing crimson to her ears, Miao Zhiyu snapped shyly: "Quit talking nonsense, Young Master Cao!"—her eyes sneaking right back to Qiao Wenxian!
Noticing her blush, Cao Ban pivoted smooth: "My cup next—time to unpack allusions!"
Downing his wine, he eyed the scroll: "The Book of Rites states tortoise shells serve for 'bu' divination and milfoil stalks for 'shi'—hence 'golden coins drop for divination'! The Zuo Commentary cites 'Wangsun' as noble scion, echoed in Wang Wei's 'a noble lord may linger'—meaning aristocrats like us, Lin Jue included! And Zheng Xuan's Han commentary on the Book of Songs defines 'zaobai' as distinguishing right from wrong—echoed in 'Why ask right from wrong?' Weaving strict character riddles and classical allusions without losing raw emotion proves Youqi Jushi's literary mastery towers over all of us!"
Every classical citation from Cao Ban drew awe—Shen Jianchen stared impressed: Did this guy swallow an entire library?!
An Mingxuan groaned laughing: "Stared ten minutes spotting zero allusions, figured you'd finally sit quiet—and you still pull three out of thin air! Hats off!"
"Read a few extra shelves with a decent memory—forgive the showing off!" Cao Ban grinned.
"Shut your trap and I'll forgive you—next time keep allusions secret so we don't look dumb!" Laughing, An Mingxuan sipped his wine: "Good vintage—worth two cents from me! Nobody turns to divination until backed against a wall—'golden coins drop' lays bare her utter helplessness! Crucially, do 'he,' 'noble lord,' 'heartless one,' and 'foe' point to one man? Worth debating! To me, 'he' and 'noble lord' mean the lost sweetheart she pines for, whereas 'heartless one' and 'foe' mean the husband she longs to escape—so I disagree with Brother Cao lumping Lin Jue under 'noble lord'!"
Holding back till last, Qiao Wenxian emptied his cup: "My turn next."
All eyes leaned forward—Wenyuan always saw deeper than anyone else.
#CH 47
#TITLE: Chapter 47: Reading Poetry, Parsing Private Lives
Gazing at "The Heartbreak Riddle" felt like beholding Zhu Shuzhen herself; Qiao Wenxian had kept silent only to picture her face and feel her ache through each stroke.
"Outwardly Youqi Jushi appears cool and reserved—far from it! Beneath the surface her passion runs storm-deep! Reading 'The Heartbreak Riddle' lets me feel the churning waves inside her—her defiance, her anguish..."
Pausing a beat, he went on: "Utter helplessness bleeds from the opening—no path to the life she longs for or the person in her heart, left tossing coins to question Heaven! Hate blooms only where love lived: 'resenting' confirms she loved deeply, only to be left behind..."
Assuming "heartless one" also pointed to Young Master Xu, gloom shadowed Wenyuan's eyes—Shuzhen still pined for him!
Voice softening with ache, he continued: "A refined daughter of noble halls, soft-spoken, always choosing delicate words—yet here she drops the harsh word 'cursing' (詈)! Not merely to fit the riddle for 'four'—brutal reality drove her past breaking into raw curses! Reading that breaks my heart and screams how cruelly mismatched her marriage is!"
Miao Zhiyu sighed soft: "A woman adapts to the husband she's handed—trapped with a crude brute like the Training Officer, Youqi Jushi had no choice but to harden."
Gazing fondly at her brother, Qiao Mengrong counted her blessings—growing up beside Brother, betrothed to him, assured of sweet harmony forever.
Sympathetic toward Zhu Shuzhen's awful match though he was, Shen Jianchen felt a twitch of discomfort—private life was private! Putting raw personal wounds into verse for crowds to dissect?
Dressed up as "literary appreciation," this felt like highbrow gossip! Thank heaven Zhu Shuzhen wasn't sitting here watching strangers rip open her scabs and rub salt in them!
Partly on her too—why pour intimate heartache into poems for her own peers AND centuries of future readers to gossip over? Sad indeed!
If he ever met Zhu Shuzhen in person, Shen Jianchen resolved to tip her off: quit spilling personal drama in public poems!
Warming to Miao Zhiyu's remark with a quick smile, Qiao Wenxian wrapped up: "'Regretting' cuts two ways—regretting losing her poise in harsh words, and regretting obeying her parents to step into that bridal sedan—yet showing us she fights tooth and nail not to let a broken match warp her soul! 'Upper union, no lower tie' exposes marriage's bleakest tragedy: binding two minds with zero bond! And her closing sweep—why ask right or wrong?—captures the saddest state on earth: a dead heart. While hope flickers, even blades leave tangled threads; once the heart dies, not a shred remains."
Silence hung heavy after Wenyuan finished; aside from Shen Jianchen, every soul grieved for Zhu Shuzhen.
Dongfang Chenze broke the hush: "Nobody reads Youqi Jushi's heart like Brother Qiao—spot-on! Any insights from Young Master Shen or Miss Qiao?"
Lifting her wine cup with poise, Qiao Mengrong smiled: "Your readings cover every nuance and echo everything I could wish to say—allow me to toast your brilliance!"
Draining her cup, she glanced toward her brother.
Uninterested in dissecting strangers' private lives—and burning to chase his lead on Qianli Xun—Shen Jianchen shifted gears: "Honored to meet you gentlemen! Poetry critique isn't my trade; tagged along with Wenyuan mainly to ask one question: do any of you three write huaben scripts for storytellers?"
All three shook their heads at once.
"We catch storytelling now and then, yet never stoop to writing lowbrow scripts," An Mingxuan replied.
"Ever heard of a scholar named Qianli Xun?" Shen Jianchen pressed.
"Never heard the name," Cao Ban shook his head.
"Wait—this Qianli Xun writes scripts for storytellers?" Dongfang Chenze asked.
"Right! Do you know him, Young Master Dongfang?" Hope flared sharp!
Dongfang Chenze shook his head: "Never met or heard of him—though fellows selling scripts to storytellers are usually down-on-their-luck exam failures scraping by! Want to track down writers like that, check Dongren Street—that's where broke scholars cluster."
A cold jolt hit Shen Jianchen! Not only might Qianli Xun have crossed into a man—she might be living hand to mouth as a broke scholar while he lived high as an imperial prince surrounded by silk and a devoted sister!
Unacceptable! He had to track Qianli Xun down fast and lift her straight into the good life!
"Much obliged for the tip, Young Master Dongfang!"
Qianli Xun... who is that? Qiao Mengrong wondered quietly—yet hearing Brother call him a male scholar melted every knot in her chest! Long as Brother wasn't chasing another woman, she'd cheer him on finding any friend!
Still steeped in Zhu Shuzhen's sorrow, Qiao Wenxian paid zero attention to Prince Yi's hunt.
Seeing the discussion close, Huayu and Nan Shuang rolled up the scroll.
Stepping beside his master, Nan Shuang whispered: "Master—you've got another brand-new lyric by Youqi Jushi too!"
Right! Distracted by "The Heartbreak Riddle," Qiao Wenxian had clean forgotten his own treasure!
Turning to the group: "I also happen to have another new lyric by Youqi Jushi—just a discarded draft, hope nobody minds!"
"Mind?! Everybody knows crowds camp outside Lin Manor's gate just hoping for her scraps! Bring it out fast!" Dongfang Chenze urged.
Shen Jianchen smirked inwardly—fighting over literal trash? Until he remembered Wenyuan's own page had scavenged it out of pure devotion—love made smart men do crazy things!
Pulling the sheet from his vest, Nan Shuang held it open; every eye spotted the black ink blot staining the character "you" (伊).
Miao Zhiyu spoke up first: "Strange... the calligraphy doesn't look quite like Youqi Jushi's hand."
Dongfang Chenze frowned closer: "Looking sharp, it really doesn't! Felt a flicker of doubt copying Lin Zixiao's scroll the other day too—hearing Lady Miao say it confirms my hunch!"
"Yeah... definitely off, can't quite put a finger on how," Cao Ban muttered.
An Mingxuan pinpointed it: "Youqi Jushi's strokes flow supple and soft—this hand tries mimicking her style, yet can't capture that softness; raw force punches straight through!"
Panicking lest they doubt his prized scoop, Nan Shuang protested fast: "Gentlemen, Lady Miao—I camped outside Lin Manor's gate for this, and Sister Kuqiao handed it straight to me! Guaranteed genuine—don't doubt it!"
Qiao Wenxian stepped in steady: "No doubt Nan Shuang brought genuine work—the voice and piercing sorrow belong unmistakably to her! Truth is, my own brows furrowed seeing this hand back in Bianliang. Returning to Lin'an, hearing from Nan Shuang she toughened up and from Lady Miao she knows martial arts—a harsh marriage transforms a person, and handwriting shifts right along with spirit!"
"Wait—just remembered!" Dongfang Chenze snapped his fingers. "Kuqiao told us the Training Officer beat her into amnesia—forgetting heaps of things! Small wonder her brushwork changed!"
"Amnesia?!" Alarm flashed across Qiao Wenxian's face. "Does she still remember us?!"
"Claimed she remembers bits—doubtful though, she didn't even recognize Chen Haoyu!"
A sharp twinge hit Wenyuan—what if she forgot him too?! Then silver lining flashed: means she forgot Young Master Xu too! Brightening fast: "Wiping the slate clean might actually be a blessing for her!"
Listening to their chatter over Youqi Jushi's twist, Shen Jianchen just pitied the talented poetess—bad match, brutal husband, knocked into amnesia—and admired her grit for holding back her martial skills instead of punching back just to get out clean!
Just then the steward and chef entered carrying steaming platters.
"Dishes are up, young masters and ladies! Take your time—Young Master Chen said stay as long as you please, even overnight, guest rooms are ready!"
"Time to eat first—we'll study Youqi Jushi's new lyric right after!" Dongfang Chenze announced.
Tucking the draft safe back inside his vest, Nan Shuang stepped aside.
Platters laid, the chef rolled in a tiered wooden serving cart overflowing with lavish courses.
Eyes popping at dishes he'd never seen, Nan Shuang jumped in helping set plates—feasting his eyes on every course and plotting how hard he'd brag to Bai Feng tonight!
At Miao Zhiyu's nod, her maid lifted a wine pot pouring rounds while Xilan grabbed the second pot to help.
Every dish arranged, the steward bowed: "Enjoy! Anything less than perfect, tell the chef and he'll remake it on the spot!"
"Much obliged to both of you—go rest, we'll call if needed," Dongfang Chenze smiled.
Steward and chef withdrew.
Eyeing a banquet lavish enough to rival—even top—imperial fare, Shen Jianchen marveled how insanely rich the Chen clan must be!
Cao Ban laughed: "Living like an emperor, Chen Haoyu! Never even seen half these dishes—eating his feast makes me feel guilty calling him a dandy next time!"
An Mingxuan scoffed: "Bought off by one spread? No backbone! I'm eating every bite AND still calling him a dandy! Besides, this settles his favor with Brother Dongfang—we owe him zilch!"
Cheering agreement, everyone clinked cups and dug in.
Attendants stood serving until their masters neared full, then took seats to finish the mountain of untouched delicacies—Nan Shuang wishing Bai Feng were there for this feast!
Dinner done, they brought out Zhu Shuzhen's lyric for another long discussion before wrapping up the salon, strolling downstairs to enjoy the riverbank breeze a spell, then boarding carriages home—unseemly staying overnight with two unmarried ladies along.
A total bust for Shen Jianchen—decided right then to skip future poetry salons with Wenyuan, not out of dislike for verse, simply tired of watching polite society dissect people's private pain!
#CH 48
#TITLE: Chapter 48: Mistaken Identity—Qian Lixun
Night had fallen by the time they returned to Youlin Retreat.
Back from West Third Alley, Bai Feng came straight to report. Spotting Prince Yi drilling forms in the yard, he stood quiet watching in awe—wondering how many years till his own fists looked half that sharp!
Catching Bai Feng out of the corner of his eye, Shen Jianchen lowered his guard crisp: "Any luck today?"
"Courier didn't show today—though I hit it off fast with the storyteller! Even invited me to dinner and lets me wait indoors!"
Shen Jianchen chuckled: "Making friends wherever you go—good, beats freezing on the curb! Normal he skipped a day; try again tomorrow."
"Yes, Your Highness! Ready to turn in?"
Wide awake still, Shen Jianchen shook his head: "Walk up to the tree platform with me."
Climbing up above the woods together, they noticed lamplight glowing through Wenyuan's window—still reading by candlelight! Guy wasn't even taking imperial exams; why grind so hard?
Then it clicked: zero phones, zero movies in the Song dynasty—books were the best entertainment going!
Gazing out through moonlight toward the Divine Willow, he saw the moon fuller than days ago—nearly round. Far off, dwarfed against dark hills yet towering inside Shen Jianchen's heart, stood the serene willow.
Every ounce of his love for Qianli Xun crossed time with him, witnessed by that willow; even their duel beneath its branches before the quake held love in every clash!
Yet if Qianli Xun now walked in a male scholar's shoes, bitterness flared toward the willow—why shatter his heart like this?
Watching his master brood silent over the treetops, Bai Feng broke the quiet: "Your Highness, the storyteller mentioned the courier looked like a country fellow, not a city servant. Could the person you're hunting live out in the countryside?"
"Countryside?" Shen Jianchen murmured—figuring if Qianli Xun was a broke scholar, of course any helper wore rough country cloth! Still, check Dongren Street first thing tomorrow! "Let's head back down."
...
Easy enough to find—Qiao Manor's coachman knew Dongren Street well and drove Shen Jianchen into town at sunrise.
Qiao Wenxian stayed holed up indoors nursing Zhu Shuzhen's heartbreak; Shen Jianchen got it—feeling the same gnawing ache worrying Qianli Xun lived in poverty!
Qiao Mengrong asked to come along, yet Shen Jianchen turned her down gentle—Dongren Street teemed with broke male scholars, no place for a princess!
Knowing Brother was looking for a male friend, Qiao Mengrong took the refusal calm without worry.
The instant they cleared the city gate, Bai Feng hopped down and sprinted straight for West Third Alley on stakeout duty.
Rolling past bustling avenues and turning east, the carriage entered ever poorer lanes.
Halting at a narrow crossroad, the driver called: "Dongren Street ahead, Young Master Shen—too tight for the carriage, gotta walk from here."
Hopping down, Shen Jianchen instructed: "Wait right here."
Narrow indeed, flanked by sagging shacks, littered with refuse and puddles reeking sour.
Not just scholars lived here—women and kids crowded the doorways too.
Grimy-faced youngsters played right amid street scraps.
Women sat on stoops wearing faded homespun—worlds apart from Qiao Mengrong or Miao Zhiyu—dull eyes, hair knotted bare without pins, weather-beaten skin untouched by powder.
Shabby scholars shuffled past eyes down, hugging worn book bundles in threadbare robes.
Heart aching at the sight, Shen Jianchen stopped one passing scholar: "Pardon me, sir—does a scholar named Qianli Xun live around here?"
Shaking his head: "Never heard of him."
Next he tried a woman sewing outside her door: "Excuse me, sister—know anyone named Qianli Xun here?"
Glancing up blank, she went straight back to her stitches without a word.
A pack of kids dashed up, one beaming cheeky: "Big Brother! Fancy clothes—you're loaded! Buy us candy?!"
An instant plan clicked! Crouching to grin at them: "Find someone named Qianli Xun for Big Brother, and I buy every single one of you mountains of treats!"
"Deal!" the leader cheered.
"Deal! Waiting right back at the crossroad—find him and run tell me!"
Like sparks from a flint, the pack scattered down every alley.
Walking back to the crossroad, Shen Jianchen handed his driver loose silver to buy candies and snacks.
Soon the driver returned with two bulging paper bags stowed on the carriage seat.
While waiting for the kids, Shen Jianchen climbed inside and shut his eyes to rest.
Soon eager shouts rang out: "Where's Big Brother?!"
Popping straight out of the carriage: "Found him?!"
"Found him! Guy named Qian Lixun!"
Thrilled yet wary, Shen Jianchen told the driver to pass out all the candies and treats.
Screaming with joy like New Year's Day, the kids stuffed their pockets while an older boy grabbed Shen Jianchen's hand: "Come on, Big Brother—I'll show you his house!"
Surrounded by chattering kids, he wound deeper down Dongren Street.
Halting before a worn door, the boy pointed: "Right here, Big Brother!"—then thumped twice on the wood with his fist.
A woman cradling an infant pulled the door open and snapped at the kids: "Who told you brats to pound on my door?!"
Giggling, the kids darted off down the lane.
Stepping up polite, Shen Jianchen asked: "Excuse me—does Qianli Xun live here?"
Sizing up his fine silks, the woman hollered inside: "Old Qian! Somebody asking for you!"
A scholarly-looking man nearing thirty stepped out clutching a book: "Who is it?"
His wife nagged sharp: "Buried in books sunrise to sunset—what good's that?! Go hawk goods on the street and feed us!"
"Guest standing right here—ease off a bit," the man murmured.
Stepping inside the tiny yard, Shen Jianchen stared hard: "Are you Qianli Xun?"
"I am indeed Qian Lixun—how may I help you, young master?"
Hearing yes blew every dam inside Shen Jianchen! Lunging forward, he wrapped both arms tight around the stunned man: "I FINALLY found you!"
Baffled stiff, the man stammered: "Hold on—I don't even know you, young master!"
Squeezing tighter, Shen Jianchen choked out: "It's me—Shen Jianchen! The Divine Willow dragged us both here! Never dreamed you ended up like this—married with a kid and all!"
Weirded out completely, the man shoved him back: "Quit hugging me—creepy! Shen who? Divine what? Haven't a clue what you're talking about!"
Staggering back, cold sense slapped Shen Jianchen awake! Wait—crossing over turned HIM into Zhao Pu; why on earth would Qianli Xun keep her exact original name?! Wait—then why did the storyteller know the name Qianli Xun?!
"Hold on—are you the Qianli Xun supplying huaben scripts to the storyteller in West Third Alley?!"
"Never written a huaben in my life—thought about trying, haven't started yet!"
"How do you write your name?!"
"Qian (千) as in a vast army, Li (离) as in leaving home, Xun (循) as in following rules!"
Pure homophone—not Qianli Xun at all!
Exhaling sheepish relief, Shen Jianchen bowed: "Wrong person—so sorry for barging in!"
Pulling out one tael of silver: "For your trouble—please accept this!"
His wife swooped in snatching the silver with a dazzling grin: "Thank you, rich generous young master!"
Relieved it wasn't her—yet bummed all over again—Shen Jianchen swore off wild goose chases before making an even bigger fool of himself!
Stick to West Third Alley! Names might match by fluke, yet NOBODY on earth wrote "Hongxiang and Xuanying" except Qianli Xun!
Meanwhile, bored stiff and starving at the storyteller's place, Bai Feng slipped out to a noodle stall, slurped down a steaming bowl of soup noodles, paid up, patted his full belly, and strolled back toward West Third Alley.
Right at the alley mouth he brushed shoulder to shoulder past Kujue without giving the tanned country lad a second glance—then rapped on the storyteller's door!
Swinging open the door, the storyteller blurted: "Courier just came and left!"
"What'd he look like?!"
"Same guy—tanned, stocky, short!"
"Name?!"
"Told you already he won't give his name! Quit talking—chase him!"
Bolting like a hound off a leash, Bai Feng realized he had just passed the exact guy at the alley mouth and sprinted straight toward the city gates!
Scanning tight through bustling crowds while weaving fast, he locked eyes onto a stocky, tanned back ahead! Closing in, he clamped an iron grip on the lad's shoulder: "Hey! You the guy who just dropped a script in West Third Alley?!"
Trapped fast—strong as Kujue was, this slender kid's iron fingers refused to budge!—"Who are you to grab me?!"
Hauling Kujue into a quiet nook without letting go, Bai Feng pressed: "Answer my question first, then I let go!"
Too honest to spin smooth lies—yet remembering the former Proprietor's strict rule never to reveal her name—Kujue hesitated before admitting: "Yeah, I dropped it."
Still holding tight: "Good—come take a quick trip with me!"
Dragging him to a carriage hire stand, Bai Feng flagged a rig and shoved Kujue inside: "Relax—I'm one of the good guys, won't hurt you! Just taking you to answer a gentleman's questions, then you're free!"
Terrified—simple-minded maybe, yet nobody stayed calm getting kidnapped out of town!—Kujue scrambled for an escape!
Seeing city walls fall behind spiked pure panic—bandits?! Gotta bolt NOW!
Squirming toward Bai Feng: "Little master... gotta pee so bad I'm bursting!"
"Trying to run, huh? Told you I'm a good guy—won't hurt you!"
Fat chance Kujue believed that! "Really can't hold it—let me step off two seconds!"
Ordering the driver to stop, Bai Feng escorted him behind roadside bushes.
Pretending to fumble his sash while eyeing Bai Feng hovering two feet away, Kujue pleaded: "Can't go with you staring right at me, little master—turn your head!"
Figuring nobody could outrun him from two feet off, Bai Feng glanced sideways.
The instant his eyes turned, Kujue tore off like a spooked deer!
Cursing, Bai Feng sprinted right behind! Bursting onto the main road inches from grabbing Kujue's collar—a passing carriage screeched to a halt, yanked Kujue straight inside, and thundered forward! Stranded, Bai Feng dashed back to his own rig yelling for his driver to give full chase!
Pure luck: that passing carriage belonged to the village head returning from town errands—spotting Kujue chased by a stranger, he scooped him straight aboard!
Gasping endless thanks inside, Kujue felt he'd just dodged death!
"Word is bandits roam outside town lately—keep your eyes peeled!" the village head warned.
Kujue bobbed his head nonstop.
Shadowing tight behind, Bai Feng watched the lead carriage vanish straight into a dense bamboo forest!
Checking with his driver: "Folks live inside that bamboo grove?"
"Yep—a bamboo-cutting village tucked inside."
"Just one village?"
"Only one! That rig's from there for sure."
Only one village—piece of cake to track down! Big breakthrough at last—no more staking out West Third Alley! Time to race back and report to His Highness!
#CH 49
#TITLE: Chapter 49: Face to Face, Yet Unrecognized
Back in the bamboo grove, Kujue hurried straight to report to the former Proprietor—by now his old nerves around her had vanished.
Returning from a morning stroll through the bamboo, Qianli Xun met him right outside her cottage steps.
"Back early today?"
Usually Kujue walked both ways, eating up half the day; today's wild ride cut hours off!
"Delivered the script, former Proprietor—weird thing happened though! Some stranger asked if I delivered the pages, and when I said yes, he tried dragging me off!"
"Huh? Really?!" Qianli Xun frowned—issues with her script? If the storyteller hated it he'd just refuse it; why grab the courier?! "Who was he? What'd he look like?"
"No idea who—sharp, pretty face, fancy clothes, crazy strong grip!"
"Did he ask my name?"
"Didn't ask—and even if he had, my lips stayed sealed! Just told the storyteller a scholar named Qianli Xun wrote it, like you instructed, not a word more."
Pleased, Qianli Xun nodded: "Good lad! Kuqiao!"
Catching her cue, Kuqiao stepped over pressing loose silver into her brother's palm: "Reward from Miss."
"Happy to run errands free—can't take coin!" Kujue tried dodging.
Shoving it firm into his fist: "Take it, or Miss gets mad!"
Tucking the silver away grinning: "Any more scripts to run, former Proprietor, just holler!"
"No more runs needed—three days left till my full month is up, then I head back to town."
Patting his pocket happy, Kujue trotted home.
One month flew by in a blink! Qianli Xun hated leaving already—almost planned to come back once her divorce settled, until remembering Chen Haoyu owned the grove now! Oh well, scratch that plan.
No magical thunderbolt had struck her destiny in this bamboo grove—yet stepping away from Lin Manor had cooled her head and sharpened her thinking. Once back, she planned to ditch fists and sit down with Lin Jue like calm adults to talk through mutual divorce.
She had also looked squarely at her sudden longing for love.
Back in her modern life, she had steered clear of romance—chasing career goals and loathing casual flings, convinced dating without marriage in mind was pure player nonsense she refused to touch, so she turned down every suitor cold.
Dying once shattered every ambition and business dream into rubble beneath that quake. Waking up in another era inside Zhu Shuzhen's body drove home how hollow fame and fortune really were—real happiness meant walking through life beside one cherished partner! Worse, waking trapped inside an unwanted marriage sharpened the ache of never having tasted real love once.
So yes—she wanted to fall in love, to find someone worth sharing the rest of this strange new life with.
Who? Every time she pictured romance, Shen Jianchen's face surfaced unbidden: Did he cross over too? Where on earth would I even find him?
No answers—maybe their paths would never cross again. Leaving love to fate, she decided: if the right man appeared—Shen Jianchen or not—she'd dive in with an open heart!
What sort of man would win her? Probably someone like Shen Jianchen—head and shoulders above every suitor she'd ever met back home—or maybe someone totally unexpected; she'd know when she met him.
Sometimes she daydreamed Shen Jianchen crossing into a dashing Song gentleman, popping out of nowhere proposing: "Qianli Xun, marry me!"—and just like her dream, she'd say yes in a heartbeat!
Stepping inside, she told Kuqiao and Xiaguo to start packing up—even clearing the study desk; done writing for the final two or three days, she planned to soak in pure bamboo serenity till departure.
Part of her hated parting with this grove so much she almost considered buying it back from Chen Haoyu—only to drop the thought fast, knowing that petty jerk would jack the price to the sky!
Next morning after breakfast, craving solo quiet, Qianli Xun wandered down a narrow bamboo trail alone, crisp dry bamboo leaves rustling underfoot.
Deep inside the grove she emerged beside a crystal mountain stream rippling carefree over smooth boulders. Settling onto a flat rock, she drank in the tranquil scene.
Mid-reverie, two men stepped across stepping-stones from the opposite bank—finely dressed, clearly outsiders, not villagers: a tall gentleman in front, a sharp-looking attendant behind.
Shen Jianchen and Bai Feng—crossing the stream and looking up straight at Qianli Xun!
Stepping forward polite, Bai Feng asked: "Miss, which way to the bamboo village?"
Suspicion flickered in Qianli Xun—ignoring the main road to sneak through back paths? Up to no good?
"Where'd you two come from? Why skip the main road into the village?"
"Rode out by carriage," Bai Feng explained smooth. "Spotted this huge bamboo forest—my young master had never seen one, got curious walking a side trail, and got turned around! Lucky we bumped into you!"
Sizing up the tall, upright young gentleman standing back—handsome features, earnest attendant, zero shady vibe—Qianli Xun asked: "What brings you to the village?"
"Looking for someone," Bai Feng replied.
"Who?"
Stuck—Bai Feng frowned: "Talking like a magistrate interrogating suspects, miss!"
Qianli Xun sniffed: "How do I know if you're honest folks or crooks?"
Calling from behind, Shen Jianchen cut in: "Drop it, Bai Feng! If she's sitting here, the village is right nearby—there's a clear path right ahead, follow it and we hit the village!"
Jogging back beside his master, Bai Feng whispered: "Think rich folks live in this forest too, Your Highness? Looks like a fancy household's maid!"
Glancing back at her plain cotton clothes, Shen Jianchen guessed: "Probably a city maid home visiting family."
"Makes sense!" Side by side, they turned straight onto the trail Qianli Xun had just walked.
"Pretty sharp," Qianli Xun murmured after them.
Returning her gaze to the clear stream, she traced its sparkling path back toward the distant green hills feeding it.
Following the woodland track straight ahead, Shen Jianchen and Bai Feng soon emerged among clustered bamboo houses.
"Found it, Your Highness!" Bai Feng cheered.
"Told you to stop calling me Your Highness—forgot again!" Shen Jianchen chided.
"Oops—got excited!" Bai Feng grinned sheepish.
"Ask around where that script courier lives."
"On it, Young Master!"
Spotting a villager hauling cut bamboo poles, Bai Feng stepped up bowing: "Uncle, hoping to ask after someone."
Halting, the villager asked: "Who?"
Spinning smooth on the spot, Bai Feng said: "Borrowed money from a guy and came to pay him back—lost his address! Know he lives here: about my age, shorter, tanned, stocky, honest look."
"Came all this way to repay a debt?"
"Yep!" Bai Feng grinned.
"Rare to see honest folks these days—for that, let me think!" Pausing a beat: "Sounds dead-on like Kujue!"
"Which bamboo house is his?!" Bai Feng lit up.
Pointing down the lane: "Straight down this path—very first super rundown bamboo shack is the Ku house! Kujue lives there!"
"Thanks a million, Uncle!"
Winding down the narrow lanes, Shen Jianchen and Bai Feng scanned left and right for the shabbiest bamboo shack.
Inside, finishing chores and stepping to his doorway, Kujue spotted Bai Feng instantly—and jerked straight back inside grabbing Kuqiao: "Sis! That kidnapper hunting the writer tracked me right here! I'm hiding—if they ask, swear you never heard of me!"
Before Kuqiao even blinked, Kujue bolted straight out the back door!
Reaching the first weathered bamboo shack, Bai Feng hollered outside: "Excuse me—does Kujue live here?!"
Dead silence. He called again: "Does Kujue live here?!"
Kuqiao stepped out onto the stoop eyeing both strangers: "Looking for who?"
"Does Kujue live here?" Bai Feng repeated.
"Never heard of him—wrong house!" Kuqiao lied flat.
Right on cue, a neighbor strolled past waving cheerful: "Hey Kuqiao—got visitors?!"
Sold out on the spot! Shooting the clueless neighbor a death glare as he wandered off baffled, Kuqiao braced herself.
Bai Feng smirked wide: "Kuqiao?! Same surname 'Ku' and never heard of Kujue?! Spill it—what's Kujue to you?!"
"Who ARE you two, hunting my little brother?!" Kuqiao bristled.
Stepping forward gentle, Shen Jianchen reassured her: "Don't panic, miss—mean your brother zero harm! Just want to know who wrote the huaben scripts he drops off for the storyteller."
Remembering Miss's strict warning never to expose her pen name—and unsure if these strangers meant trouble—Kuqiao played stone wall:
"Why do you care?"
"Is the author's name Qianli Xun?" Shen Jianchen pressed eager.
Dodging straight answers, Kuqiao improvised smooth: "Sure, my brother ran scripts and gave the name Scholar Qianli Xun—too bad you're a step late! He already packed up and left, no idea where!"
Every spark crashed out of Shen Jianchen's eyes: "What?! Left?! Where did he go?!"
"Told you—no idea where!"
"When did he leave?!"
"Three days ago!"
"Which direction?!"
"So many questions, young master! Qianli Xun drifted in out of nowhere and drifted out just as fast! Barely knew him—my brother just took coin to run errands, know nothing else!"
Calm face, airtight story—deflated to the core, Shen Jianchen bowed heavy: "Forgive the intrusion, miss." Turning, he walked off shoulders slumped.
No point lingering; shooting Kuqiao a parting "Hmph!" over his shoulder, Bai Feng hurried after his master.
Rolling her eyes right back, Kuqiao beamed proud of herself for brushing off two strangers clean!
Slipping back in through the back door, Kujue peeked out front: "Gone, Sis?"
"Your big sister handled it in two seconds!" puffing up proud before reminding him: "Parents are away visiting kin, and I leave in two days too—take good care of yourself!"
Grinning sheepish: "Almost a married man—I can handle myself!"
"Good to hear!" Heading back to the kitchen, Kuqiao set to cooking her signature bamboo tube rice—only a few country meals left for Miss!
Walking glum toward the village exit beside silent Bai Feng, Shen Jianchen felt his sole lead crumble to ash—where on earth to hunt Qianli Xun now?
Right near the village edge, they crossed paths once more with Qianli Xun strolling back from the bamboo stream.
Recognizing each other from minutes earlier, Shen Jianchen offered a polite nod and faint smile; Qianli Xun smiled politely back—walking clean past each other, missing their very first chance at reunion by inches!
#CH 50
#TITLE: Chapter 50: A Calm Talk of Mutual Divorce
Steaming hot bamboo tube rice packed in her basket, Kuqiao carried lunch back to the cottage and set it out—writing gear and tea utensils all packed away now, leaving the low study table pure dining table.
Settling down to enjoy her meal, Kuqiao noticed Xiaguo missing: "Where's Xiaguo, Miss?"
"Heading back to town soon—sent her home to say goodbye to her family."
"You spoil Xiaguo, Miss."
"Spoil you just as much!"
"Serving you is lifetimes of luck for both of us!" Remembering the morning's surprise visitors, Kuqiao added: "Oh—two strangers came hunting Kujue today asking after Qianli Xun!"
Qianli Xun froze mid-scoop with her bamboo spoon! Who in this dynasty knew her real name?! Could it be Shen Jianchen?! Heart leaping, she blurted: "Did they say who they were?!"
Kuqiao shook her head: "Never said—too busy shooing them off, forgot to ask."
"Why were they hunting Qianli Xun?"
"You left the pen name Qianli Xun with the storyteller—bet that gentleman loved hearing the story and tracked the courier hoping to meet the author! Kept my mouth locked tight, told them Qianli Xun left the grove days ago to parts unknown! Poor guy looked crushed and walked right off."
Wait—connecting the dots to the two strangers by the mountain stream: "Tall, handsome gentleman with a sharp-looking attendant?"
"Yep! Ran into them, Miss?"
Instantly Qianli Xun bought Kuqiao's theory—no way could that be Shen Jianchen, coincidence wasn't THAT wild! Resuming her bamboo tube rice: "Yeah—even asked me directions."
"My Miss is unstoppable!" Kuqiao beamed. "Folks camp at the gate stealing your poetry scraps, and one week writing huaben brings fans chasing you to the woods! Were you a man, you'd have friends across the whole empire—beat Young Master Qiao hands down!"
Smiling silent, Qianli Xun savored her country meal, wondering what awaited her back in Lin'an once this peaceful month closed.
Two days flew by; right on schedule, Lin Manor's coachman waited at the village entrance.
Kujue, the village head, and a warm knot of villagers gathered to see her off.
Waving goodbye to all, Qianli Xun boarded the carriage for the road back to town.
Only an hour back to Lin'an by carriage—barely two hours on foot for Kujue—yet in Qianli Xun's heart, Lin Manor sat light-years apart from her bamboo haven.
Pulling up outside Lin Manor, Matron Wei stood waiting at the gate with several page boys who hustled forward to unload luggage the moment Qianli Xun and her maids stepped down.
"Had Zhulan Residence aired and scrubbed spotless—rest from the ride before touching brush or ink," Matron Wei welcomed warm.
Having someone genuinely look out for her wrapped Qianli Xun in quiet comfort. Nodding thanks, she asked: "Is Lin Jue home?"
"Not back yet—first time in history you walked through the gate asking after Son-in-law!" Matron Wei teased.
Leaning near Matron Wei's ear, Qianli Xun whispered: "Eager to bring up mutual divorce right away."
"No rush—from what I hear, mutual divorce is already warming up! Come inside and I'll tell you everything." Linking arms, Matron Wei walked her in.
Inside Zhulan Residence, Qianli Xun slipped into comfortable robes while Kuqiao naturally set to whisking tea with Xiaguo watching beside her to learn.
"Step outside a moment, Xiaguo—need a word with our young lady," Matron Wei directed.
Resentment pricked Xiaguo—personal maid too, why always boot HER out while Kuqiao stayed pouring tea?! Swallowing her pride, she murmured: "Yes, Matron."
Once Xiaguo stepped out, Matron Wei sat down: "Getting back to Lin Manor, I heard from servants that Eldest Master Lin and Second Master Lin sat Son-in-law down over wine and talked hard sense into him about mutual divorce! He isn't digging his heels in like before—actually swayed, told his brothers he'd think it over for a few days!"
Bamboo luck REALLY worked! Qianli Xun silently sent a thousand thanks up the hill to Master Jingguang!
"Hope he doesn't 'think it over' forever!"
"Even better—word is Son-in-law plans on taking a concubine!"
"He can take ten concubines for all I care!"
"You SHOULD care—taking a concubine proves he's finally letting you go!"
"How so?"
"Never cared for Son-in-law myself, yet I saw plain he truly loved you—even hauling courtesans home to spite you, he refused to take a proper concubine! Pity you two didn't fit. Now choosing to take a concubine proves he's ready to move on from you."
Thrilled by Matron Wei's insight, Qianli Xun jumped on the opening: "Matron, order the kitchen to whip up fine dishes and good wine—I'm hosting Lin Jue right here in Zhulan Residence tonight to talk mutual divorce calm and friendly!"
"Eager as you are, say no more—I'll arrange it at once!"
Rising to head out, Matron Wei spotted Xiaguo idling aimless in the courtyard and frowned hard.
Xiaguo bobbed a quick curtsy: "Good day, Matron."
Calm yet razor-sharp, Matron Wei warned: "Remember Miss's kindness taking you in and serve with your whole heart. City life isn't a country village—watch every word and every step; never speak out of turn, never cross a line."
"Yes, Matron."
Giving her one last piercing look, Matron Wei strode out of Zhulan Residence.
Staring after Matron Wei's back, bitter resentment festered inside Xiaguo—why did that old woman treat her like dirt every single day?! Done zero wrong—just punished for having a prettier face?! Worried Miss or Kuqiao might read resentment on her face, she stepped out for a walk to cool off.
Slipping past Zhulan Residence and out of the West Courtyard, Xiaguo drifted down Lin Manor's plain lanes—nothing but Caesarweed along the dirt paths, duller than the countryside!
Comparing herself to Miss—every bit as pretty, why was fate so wildly lopsided?! Born poor, chased by Chen Haoyu, rescued as a maid only to get bullied by Matron Wei! Eyes misting with self-pity, she paused staring down at pink Caesarweed blooms.
Returning from the Military Training Office, Lin Jue caught sight of a slender silhouette from behind—thinking Zhu Shuzhen had come home at last! One step closer he saw maid's garb—who knew Lin Manor had a maid with a figure this graceful?! Stepping up, he asked: "New around here?"
Spinning round at a man's voice, Xiaguo curtsied low: "Miss's new maid hired in the countryside—my name is Xiaguo."
The instant she lifted her face, Lin Jue went breathless—every ounce as breathtaking as Zhu Shuzhen, maybe even softer! Tongue-tied, that exact bolt of lightning from his very first glimpse of Zhu Shuzhen hit his chest all over again!
Silence stretched; head bowed low, spotting fine robes telling her he was a master of the house, Xiaguo risked a timid murmur: "Master..."
"Don't call me Master—call me Lord," Lin Jue spoke at last, pivoting brisk toward the West Courtyard.
Daring a peek at his receding back, Xiaguo whispered: "Lord? That's Miss's husband?!"
Waiting till he entered the West Courtyard, Xiaguo slipped back into Zhulan Residence.
Stepping out of the room, Kuqiao spotted her and called sharp: "Hunted everywhere for you—where'd you vanish to?!"
"Helped a sweeping maid a moment, got held up."
"You're Miss's personal maid—tend Miss alone, leave sweeping to sweeping maids!"
"Yes."
Scolded by Kuqiao too—stinging resentment dug deeper inside Xiaguo, hidden fast behind an obedient mask as she stepped indoors.
Meanwhile Kuqiao marched over to Lin Jue's quarters and called in: "Son-in-law, Miss invites you to Zhulan Residence for dinner shortly."
A page boy stepped out: "Lord says come inside."
Sipping tea at his table, Lin Jue asked as Kuqiao entered: "What time did you get back today?"
"The Hour of the Sheep, Son-in-law."
"When did your Miss pick up a new maid?"
"Hired her out in the countryside, Son-in-law."
"Fine. Tell Zhu Shuzhen I'll be over in a bit."
Once Kuqiao left, checking the hour, Lin Jue changed into fresh robes and walked over to Zhulan Residence, where Kuqiao and Xiaguo stood waiting outside the door.
Casting a pleased glance at Xiaguo before stepping inside, he found a lavish table laden with dishes and fine wine—and Zhu Shuzhen seated smiling! A dizzy flash hit him: had a month away changed her mind about staying together?!
"Barred me from setting foot in Zhulan Residence—why invite me to dine now?"
"Times change—people think clearer with space. Invited you tonight to talk things over calm, like friends."
Taking a seat, Lin Jue lifted the wine pot and poured both cups brimful: "Friends it is—drink first, talk after!"
Raising her cup crisp: "Cheers!"
Setting down her empty cup, Qianli Xun spoke level and sincere: "Spent a whole month in the country thinking through us. Neither of us is evil—just two totally mismatched people shoved under one roof..."
Not making up after all! Suppressing a silent sigh, Lin Jue kept listening.
"Marry a wife who adores and respects you, and I bet you'd never raise a fist; marry a man who fits my heart, and I'd find peace too. Let me go—not for me, for YOUR own sake! Folks say look out for yourself first—trapping me here brings you zero joy!"
Lin Jue snorted: "Holding you here? Easy—I just take a concubine and live fine, while you stay stuck in Lin Manor till you die!"
"Wrong! Keep dragging your feet, and I buy my own house outside and move straight out of Lin Manor to live how I please—and whatever wild headlines I make outside shaming the Lin name will be on YOUR head!"
Funny enough, not a drop of anger heated her blood—and though Lin Jue sounded cool, that nasty bully glare had vanished too.
Already warming toward mutual divorce anyway, her blunt words didn't spark rage—yet no way was he letting her walk off empty-handed!
Refilling his cup and tossing it back clean, he spoke smooth: "Fine—I'll let you go..."
Qianli Xun lit up: "A gentleman's word stands solid!"
"Hold on—not finished! I grant mutual divorce on ONE condition."
"Name it!"
"That new maid of yours caught my eye—hand her over to me, and I sign the mutual divorce!"
Qianli Xun hesitated: "Have to ask Xiaguo first—if she agrees, great; if she says no, my hands are tied."
"She's YOUR maid—if you decide to give her to me, she can't say no!" Eyeing the untouched feast, he rose: "Fine dishes—shame I lost my appetite. Enjoy without me."
Pausing at the threshold, he eyed Xiaguo again: "Your name is Xiaguo?"
"Yes, Lord."
"Real looker! Hehehe!" Chuckling low, Lin Jue strolled out of Zhulan Residence.
#CH 51
#TITLE: Chapter 51: Xiaguo Refuses to Be a Concubine
Not a single dish touched by Lin Jue—and his parting demand killed Qianli Xun's appetite cold too! Calling toward the doorway: "You two come inside!"
Stepping in, Kuqiao and Xiaguo gladly obeyed her gesture to sit and eat alongside her, picking up chopsticks right away.
Eyeing Xiaguo, Qianli Xun wrestled with how to broach it—trading a maid for her own freedom felt awfully shady!
Still, Lin Jue was Lin'an's Training Officer from a respectable house; for a poor country girl like Xiaguo, becoming his concubine might mean security for life.
Biting the bullet, she spoke gentle: "Xiaguo, need to ask your honest opinion on something..."
Setting down her chopsticks, Xiaguo answered attentive: "Speak, Miss."
"Well... just talked things over with Lin Jue—he agreed to grant me a mutual divorce, on one condition: that I give you to him..."
Lowering her lashes, Xiaguo asked quiet: "As Lord's concubine?"
Qianli Xun nodded: "Once I leave, you'd be the only woman beside him—who knows, he might even promote you to primary wife someday!"
Eyes still downcast, Xiaguo murmured: "Don't sugarcoat it, Miss—I'm a country girl with nothing going for me except my face. How could a concubine ever get promoted to primary wife?"
Dropping straight to her knees on the floorboards, she pleaded: "My grandfather always taught me: rather be a poor man's wife than a rich man's concubine! Please don't hand me over to Lord, Miss!"
Seeing her kneel tied Qianli Xun in tighter knots: "Get up—sit back down and talk while we eat."
Iron-firm: "Until Miss promises, I'm staying on my knees!"
Kuqiao jumped in nudging her: "You called yourself a country girl—becoming Son-in-law's concubine is a huge step up! Stop putting Miss in a bind!"
Xiaguo fired right back sharp: "If Sister Kuqiao thinks being a concubine is so grand, why don't YOU go?!"
"Too bad Son-in-law didn't pick me! Had he asked for me, I'd say yes in a heartbeat—just to buy Miss her mutual divorce!" Kuqiao shot back.
Xiaguo let out a bitter laugh: "Easy talking big when it isn't your neck on the block! If Lord really picked you, bet you wouldn't gamble your whole life away!"
Seeing Xiaguo dead set against it, Qianli Xun couldn't bear forcing her—what if Xiaguo ended up Zhu Shuzhen number two, beaten behind closed doors? Qianli Xun's conscience would eat her alive forever!
Just as she opened her mouth to promise she'd never hand her over, Matron Wei's voice cut in from the doorway: "Were Kuqiao in your shoes, she WOULD say yes for our young lady—without blinking!"
"Sit and eat with us, Matron," Qianli Xun invited.
Taking a chair, Matron Wei said: "Already ate in my room—bumped into Son-in-law walking out, chatted two words, heard his demand, and came straight over."
Turning stern eyes onto Xiaguo: "Miss saved you from ruin—here's your chance to repay her! Being Son-in-law's concubine won't shortchange you one bit; on Miss's behalf, I'll even pack you a generous dowry so you walk in a rich concubine!"
Xiaguo thought suspicious: Miss can't run fast enough from this husband and wants to dump him on ME? Bet he's got some nasty hidden illness! No way am I tossing my whole future away—with my looks I can aim way higher! Plus I just hit the big city—haven't even tasted life yet!
"Precisely out of gratitude for Miss saving me, I swore never to marry and stay serving Miss for life! Don't want any dowry, Matron Wei—well fed and clothed by Miss's side as a maid is all I ever want!"
Before Matron Wei could scold further, Qianli Xun stepped in: "If Xiaguo says no, we don't force her! Get up now—you and Kuqiao pack up this food and eat at your leisure in the maids' quarters. Need quiet to think."
Thanking Miss profusely, Xiaguo rose and helped Kuqiao pack every dish into two food carriers for the maids' quarters.
Alone again, Matron Wei sighed: "Too softhearted, young lady—can't stand seeing anyone unhappy! Still, don't let Xiaguo fool you: claims she wants to serve you forever, yet deep down I bet she's angling to marry primary wife into an even richer house!"
"Can't blame her for wanting better! Force her into Lin Jue's bed unwilling and watch him beat her like he beat me? I'd never sleep at peace!"
"By rights a maid's marriage rests wholly in her mistress's hands—not her place to protest! Only because you're so kind do you weigh her wishes. Fine—whatever you choose, I back you! I'll go give Son-in-law our answer right now."
"Thank you, Matron."
Once Matron Wei left, Qianli Xun sat brooding—mutual divorce inches away, only for Xiaguo to snag the whole deal! Had she never hired Xiaguo, Lin Jue wouldn't have this card to block her—just like Matron Wei warned back in the bamboo grove!
Walking over to the main hall to call on Lin Jue, Matron Wei commanded quiet respect even from him—sharp-tongued, razor-sighted, sharper than most men; back when he beat Zhu Shuzhen, he always checked Matron Wei was out first, because one piercing glare from her shriveled his nerve!
Inviting her to sit while dodging direct eye contact lest she read straight through him, Lin Jue asked: "What brings you over, Matron?"
"Just bringing our young lady's reply—Xiaguo wants to serve Miss lifelong and refuses to marry, so please look elsewhere for a concubine."
The reply hit Lin Jue like a brick—why was his love life cursed?! Fell for Zhu Shuzhen, wed her, miserable every day till she demanded divorce; finally spotted a gorgeous maid who lit the same spark, and rejected flat again! How humiliating!
Tempers flaring past his usual fear of Matron Wei's eyes, he snapped: "Then mutual divorce is off the table!"
Having seen this coming miles away, Matron Wei rolled out her counter smooth: "Also came to give you fair warning, Son-in-law—mutual divorce or not makes zero difference! I've already picked out a fine courtyard house outside and put down deposit. Agree or refuse, our young lady moves out soon!"
Ice water poured straight down Lin Jue's spine: "Bought a house already?! Does Father-in-law know?! Pull that stunt and what happens to Zhu Manor's honor?!"
"Once Zhu Manor wed their daughter into Lin Manor, Master Zhu's opinion stopped mattering! She's a Lin wife now—moving out doesn't shame the Zhu house, it shames the LIN house!"
Every syllable hit dead-center in Lin Jue's chest—yet daring not explode at Matron Wei, he pleaded tight: "All I ask is one maid—why won't Zhu Shuzhen humor me?! Hand over Xiaguo and everyone wins—isn't that right, Matron? Talk her round—just hand Xiaguo over!"
Matron Wei sighed: "Our young lady would gladly agree—Xiaguo's the one balking, and Miss's kind heart refuses to force anyone! Tell you what, Son-in-law—why not spend some time getting close to Xiaguo on your own? Spark some affection between you two, and she'll say yes on her own!"
Lin Jue's face lit up at once: "Brilliant idea, Matron! I'll try that! Only—don't move out so fast, or how do I get time to warm that girl up?"
"Naturally!" Matron Wei smiled smooth.
Back in Zhulan Residence, mulling over Master Jingguang's four words—Right Thought and Right Mindfulness—Qianli Xun felt peace settle back: building her own freedom on another girl's misery was wrong; honoring Xiaguo's choice felt clean. Mind clear, she sat at her desk and resumed writing huaben scripts.
Kuqiao walked in holding an invitation card: "Miss, Young Master Qiao is back from Bianliang! Heard you returned from the countryside and sent Nan Shuang over with an invitation to a poetry salon!"
Setting down her brush and opening the card, Qianli Xun read: Sightseeing Pavilion by West Lake, signed Qiao Wenxian. Zhu Shuzhen's prized confidant—of course she had to attend! Date: tomorrow at noon.
"Tell the messenger I'll be there on time tomorrow."
"Already told him yes for you, Miss!"
Qianli Xun arched a brow: "Since when do you make my schedule?"
"Whenever Young Master Qiao invites, you never say no—always go! Sometimes I catch myself wishing you and Young Master Qiao ended up together..."
"Didn't you say my heart belonged to Young Master Xu? Now shipping me with Young Master Qiao?!"
"Rooted for Young Master Xu before—he married someone else, so Young Master Qiao's next up!"
"Quit daydreaming! Still got a husband blocking the way—fantasizing over other men gets you labeled lacking wifely virtue, and you dare say that aloud? Asking for a swat!"
Kuqiao giggled fearless: "Swat me all you like, still wishing you the best! Divorce is only a matter of time—in my book, reborn Miss is already a free woman, so I'm dreaming ahead for you!"
Not just Kuqiao—ever since transmigrating into Zhu Shuzhen, Qianli Xun had never treated this bogus marriage as real either; in her own heart she felt free as air!
Meanwhile, once Matron Wei left, Lin Jue plotted how to warm Xiaguo up until she willingly became his concubine. Respectable girls took such tedious effort—nothing like easy courtesans! Still, remembering Xiaguo's figure and face matching Zhu Shuzhen's beauty, putting in work felt worth every ounce!
Calling a servant in: "Fetch Madam's new maid Xiaguo—say I have questions for her."
Moments later Xiaguo arrived, already guessing his angle and ready to shut him down cold for good.
Stopping outside the main hall doorway, she called in: "You sent for me, Lord?"
"Come inside," Lin Jue answered.
Stepping across the threshold, Xiaguo kept her eyes pinned to her toes.
Liking her more by the second, Lin Jue stood and circled around her eyeing every inch top to bottom.
Skin crawling with dread that he might get handsy, Xiaguo asked stiff: "What did Lord wish to ask?"
Halting right in front of her, Lin Jue cut straight to the chase: "Why won't you come to me?"
Just as she expected! Xiaguo answered flat: "My humble birth isn't worthy of Lord..."
"Doesn't bother me."
"I refuse to be anyone's concubine."
Pausing a beat, Lin Jue pitched: "I'm divorcing Zhu Shuzhen soon—no primary wife left! Serve me well as concubine, keep me happy, and maybe I promote you to primary wife!"
"If I marry, I enter legally wed as head matriarch—never starting as concubine waiting on scraps! Besides, how much weight does Lord's word even carry?"
Impressed by her grit yet refusing to wed a peasant girl as primary wife—zero background, sheer pretty face; marrying her straight after divorcing Zhu Shuzhen would ignite clan uproar and cost him face—he tried bargaining patient: "Tell you what—I promise to promote you to primary wife in three years!"
Startled by "three years," Xiaguo snapped her head up glaring straight at him: "Three years?! Playing me for a fool, Lord?! Who knows how many women pass through your bed in three years—think you'll remember one promise? Young as I am, I'm not a three-year-old buying fairy tales!"
Her flashing eyes and striking features lit a fire straight in Lin Jue's gut; losing every ounce of restraint, he swept her tight into his arms: "Just be mine—I'll never mistreat you! Step into the inner room right now and feel how good I am to you!"
Strong as a Training Officer was, his grip locked Xiaguo helpless as he planted two sloppy kisses across her cheek!
Throwing all caution aside, he hoisted her off her feet and carried her toward the inner chamber!
Pounding and clawing at his chest, Xiaguo screamed: "Let me go! Let me go right now!..."
Fat chance Lin Jue let go—burning to claim from Xiaguo every pleasure Zhu Shuzhen had denied him!
When Zhu Shuzhen wed him, she stayed glacial, threatened suicide, and had the Prefect of Lin'an backing her, keeping Lin Jue in check; after knocking her out, she woke knowing lethal boxing he couldn't match, putting her way out of reach!
Xiaguo had zero backing, soft and fragile—and best of all, blessed with beauty matching Zhu Shuzhen's, igniting pure possessive frenzy in him!
Dropping Xiaguo onto the bed, he pinned her flat beneath his weight and leaned in for another kiss—when Xiaguo drove a vicious kick straight into his groin! Blinding agony doubled him off the bed clutching himself howling: "Aargh! Aargh!..."
Seizing the split second, Xiaguo bolted straight out the door!

## 译法建议
审校说明：已完成 50 章（第 1、3–51 章，共 3,856 段）单遍终稿与 1:1 行数核对；同步归一第 37 章原文错字「乔文轩」→ `Qiao Wenxian`、修正第 13 章错字「和理」→ `mutual divorce`、第 33 章错字「火炕」→ `living hell`；前 3 章（第 1、3 章）严格不加背景介绍或 `[TN:]` 注，第 5 章首现「和离」已作 ≤12 词内嵌解释。
和离 | heli—a divorce by mutual consent（首现）；后文 mutual divorce | 严格遵循规则首现保留独立制度词并附 ≤12 词内嵌解释，后续自然用 mutual divorce。
赵太丞家 | Grand Physician Zhao's Clinic | 第 37 章明言“原来是家医铺”，原表 `Grand Physician Zhao's Residence` 偏离实义。
火炕（第33章） | living hell | 原文明显为“火坑”笔误，原表直译 `Fire Pit` 失真。
二爷 | Second Master | 修正原表把 `二爷` 误标为 `First Master` 的冲突。
后门 | back gate / back door | 修正原表把 `后门` 误标为 `back courtyard` 的错误。
花骨朵 / 荷叶 | lotus buds / plum buds / lotus leaves | 修正原表多处被误填为 `Lake` 的错条。
亲家姑娘 | my dear in-law | 原表 `Affinal family's daughter` 过于生硬，对话用口语化称呼更自然。
脚店 | Foot Shop (Jiaodian) | 保留第 32 章男主将“脚店”误认成足浴店（foot-massage parlor）的双关笑点。
幽林别居 / 聚香楼 / 醉云楼 | Youlin Retreat / Juxiang Tower / Zuiyun Tower | 遵循甲档“音译专名+通用词意译”并与第 91–150 章译法统一。
节度使 / 练兵官 | Military Commissioner / Training Officer | 替换生僻音译 `Jiedushi` 并与后文军衔体系统一。
丫鬟 | maid | 较原表 `maidservant` 更轻快地道，符合网文阅读节奏。
海涵 | be magnanimous and forgive | 较原表 `to show forbearance` 更贴合古人请托语境。
闺阁 | inner chambers | 避免原表 `boudoir` 的西化香艳偏差。
渣男 / 母老虎 / 母夜叉 | scumbag / tigress / she-demon | 精准区分现代槽词与古人口语，避免与“花心大萝卜”混译。
休书 | bill of divorce / bill of repudiation | 凸显单方休妻与“和离（mutual divorce）”的制度差异。
老娘（女主自称） | 按语境译作 I / me 等强语气口语 | 避免机械套用 `Yours truly` 削弱女主的现代泼辣人设。
